### [1] [Flux Evolution of PSR B0540--69 and the Spectral Energy Distribution of its Pulsar Wind Nebula](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13182)
*Juan Zhang,Xuezhi Liu,Junyang Zhang,Hanlong Peng,Mingyu Ge,Songzhan Chen,Shanshan Weng,Shijie Zheng,Shumei Jia,Chengkui Li,Haisheng Zhao,Ju Guan,Yong Chen,Liming Song,Fangjun Lu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: PSR B0540--69 在 2011 年自转减慢率转变后，其 PSR$+$PWN 系统的 X 射线增亮持续超过 14 yr 至 2026 年初且源于 PWN，而脉冲 X 射线流量不变、2008--2025 年 $\gamma$ 射线流量无长期变化，表明额外自转减慢能量持续注入 PWN；其宽波段谱能量分布与蟹状星云显著不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSR B0540--69 于 2011 年 12 月发生自转减慢率转变（SRT），此后其脉冲星风星云（PWN）被报道增亮至 2018 年。需要延长 X 射线与 $\gamma$ 射线流量演化的研究时间基线，以检验增亮是否持续、是否来自 PWN，并约束 SRT 的能量收支与物理机制。

Method: 结合多个 X 射线仪器的观测与 Fermi Large Area Telescope 的数据，将 X 射线和 $\gamma$ 射线流量演化研究扩展至 2026 年，构建跨越二十余年的 PSR$+$PWN 系统流量历史；将测得的非脉冲 X 射线与 $\gamma$ 射线流量与先前发表的射电到紫外数据结合，构建 PWN 的宽波段谱能量分布。

Result: PSR$+$PWN 系统增强的 X 射线光度在 SRT 后持续超过 14 yr，直至 2026 年初，而脉冲 X 射线流量在所有历元保持不变，证实增亮源自 PWN。$\gamma$ 射线波段中脉冲和非脉冲流量在 2008 年至 2025 年间均无可探测的长期变化。所构建的 PWN 宽波段谱能量分布显示其性质与蟹状星云显著不同。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，SRT 时释放的额外自转减慢能量已在数十年间持续注入 PWN，从而对能量收支和该转变的物理机制给出强约束；PWN 的宽波段谱能量分布性质与蟹状星云显著不同。根据摘要无法判断该结果对脉冲星、磁星或快速射电暴（FRB）研究的更广泛适用性。

Abstract: The young Crab-like pulsar PSR B0540--69, located in the Large Magellanic Cloud, underwent a spin-down rate transition (SRT) in 2011 December, after which its pulsar wind nebula (PWN) was reported to brighten up to 2018. In this work, we extend the study of its X-ray and $γ$-ray flux evolution through 2026 by combining observations from multiple X-ray instruments with data from the Fermi Large Area Telescope, thereby constructing a flux history of the PSR$+$PWN system spanning more than two decades. We find that the enhanced X-ray luminosity of the PSR$+$PWN system has persisted for more than 14 yr after the SRT, up to early 2026, whereas the pulsed X-ray flux remains unchanged across all epochs. This confirms that the brightening originates from the PWN. In the $γ$-ray band, neither the pulsed nor the non-pulsed flux shows any detectable long-term variation between 2008 and 2025. Together, these results imply that the additional spin-down energy released at the SRT has been continuously injected into the PWN over decades, placing strong constraints on the energy budget and physical mechanism of the transition. Combining the non-pulsed X-ray and $γ$-ray fluxes measured here with previously published radio-to-UV data, we further construct a broadband spectral energy distribution of the PWN, which shows properties markedly different from those of the Crab Nebula.

</details>

*R. Alfaro,C. Alvarez,E. Anita-Rangel,M. Araya,J. C. Arteaga-Velázquez,D. Avila Rojas,H. A. Ayala Solares,R. Babu,P. Bangale,A. Bernal,T. Capistrán,A. Carramiñana,S. Casanova,U. Cotti,J. Cotzomi,S. Coutiño de León,E. De la Fuente,N. Di Lalla,R. Diaz Hernandez,M. A. DuVernois,J. C. Díaz-Vélez,C. Espinoza,N. Fraija,S. Fraija,A. Galván-Gámez,J. A. García-González,F. Garfias,N. Ghosh,A. Gonzalez Muñoz,M. M. González,J. A. González,J. A. Goodman,J. Gyeong,J. P. Harding,I. Herzog,D. Huang,F. Hueyotl-Zahuantitla,P. Hüntemeyer,A. Iriarte,S. Kaufmann,D. Kieda,K. Leavitt,W. H. Lee,H. León Vargas,A. L. Longinotti,G. Luis-Raya,K. Malone,O. Martinez,J. Martínez-Castro,J. A. Matthews,P. Miranda-Romagnoli,P. E. Mirón-Enriquez,J. A. Morales-Soto,E. Moreno,M. Mostafá,M. Najafi,L. Nellen,M. U. Nisa,R. Noriega-Papaqui,M. Osorio-Archila,E. Ponce,Y. Pérez Araujo,E. G. Pérez-Pérez,C. D. Rho,D. Rosa-González,M. Roth,A. Sandoval,J. Serna-Franco,M. Shin,A. J. Smith,Y. Son,R. W. Springer,O. Tibolla,I. Torres,R. Torres-Escobedo,L. Villaseñor,X. Wang,Z. Wang,I. J. Watson,H. Wu,S. Yu,S. Yun-Cárcamo,X. Zhang,H. Zhou,C. de León*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 使用 3071 天 HAWC 数据的专项分析解决了 DA 495 区域此前 VHE 观测的不一致，识别出三个具弯曲谱的延展源（HAWC J1952+2924/DA 495、HAWC J1954+2837、HAWC J1958+2848），并提示可能存在第四个源；DA 495 的多波段分析支持轻子主导的 PWN 解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DA 495 区域包含脉冲星风云 DA 495 及其他甚高能伽马射线发射体，但此前不同 VHE 实验的观测结果不一致，包括 DA 495 测得通量的差异和所报告源数量的差异，因此需要专门的观测分析来厘清该区域的源数目与性质。

Method: 使用 3071 天的 HAWC 数据进行专项分析。对 DA 495 开展了多波段分析，纳入本研究结果以及射电、X 射线和其他伽马射线数据。摘要未说明具体数据处理流程、能段划分、显著性阈值或统计分析细节。

Result: 识别出三个延展源：HAWC J1952+2924（DA 495）、HAWC J1954+2837、HAWC J1958+2848，三者均具有弯曲谱，并发现可能存在第四个源的迹象。DA 495 的延展发射在多波段分析中最好由轻子相互作用描述，推断磁场为 $B\sim10~μ\textrm{G}$，不需要强子成分。另外两个源没有已确认对应体。

Conclusion: 作者认为新分析解决了此前 VHE 实验结果的不一致。DA 495 被解释为轻子主导的 PWN，其延展发射由轻子相互作用描述且无需强子成分；另外两个无已确认对应体的源被解释为分别与附近脉冲星 PSR J1954+2836 和 PSR J1958+2846 相关的 TeV 晕，从而为不断增长的 TeV 晕族群增加两个候选体。对脉冲星单脉冲、模式转换、计时、偏振、射电谱或 FRB 的直接联系，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Previous observations of the DA 495 region, which contains the pulsar wind nebula (PWN) DA 495 and other very-high-energy (VHE; $>100~\textrm{GeV}$) gamma-ray emitters, showed inconsistent results across different VHE experiments, including differences in the measured flux of DA 495 and in the number of reported sources. Our new dedicated analysis using 3071 days of HAWC data resolves these discrepancies. We identify three extended sources, HAWC J1952+2924 (DA 495), HAWC J1954+2837, and HAWC J1958+2848, all with curved spectra, and find a hint of a fourth source. For DA 495, a multi-wavelength analysis, including our results, radio, X-ray, and other gamma-ray data, reveals that its extended emission is best described by leptonic interactions with an inferred magnetic field of $B\sim10~μ\textrm{G}$ and that hadronic components are not required. On the other hand, we interpret the other two sources, which have no confirmed counterparts, as TeV halos associated with nearby pulsars, PSR J1954+2836 and PSR J1958+2846, respectively. These results reinforce the interpretation of DA 495 as a leptonic-dominated PWN and add two candidates to the growing population of TeV halos.

</details>


### [3] [Adiabatic evolution of hadronic neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.13926)
*Domenico Logoteta,Francesco Zeno Constanzo,Ignazio Bombaci,Sebastiano Bernuzzi,Albino Perego,David Radice*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文用有限温度β稳定强子EOS计算静态与旋转中子星的结构性质，模型与PSR J0348+0432、PSR J0740+6620的质量及GW170817的潮汐形变率一致，并给出解释PNS或BNSM遗迹长期演化的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心坍缩超新星形成的前中子星或双中子星并合遗迹的长期演化需要跨越动力学模拟无法覆盖的时间尺度；同时超子单粒子势存在不确定性，需要借助超核与超核核物质实验数据约束模型。摘要以此为背景，旨在对这类致密天体的长期演化提供认识。

Method: 摘要说明采用有限温度β稳定EOS，扩展Fortin等人（2017）的零温强子EOS，考虑超子形成与中微子俘获组分；在Hartree近似下用相对论平均场量子多体理论推导EOS，耦合常数由超核与超核物质实验数据确定；计算静态与旋转（刚性及较差转动）中子星的结构性质，并给出演化方案。具体观测、频段、样本与统计方法摘要未说明。

Result: 摘要报告EOS模型对核子组分和超子组分均再现冷中子星与PNS的典型特征；与已测量质量2.01 M_sun（PSR J0348+0432）和2.14 M_sun（PSR J0740+6620）以及GW170817的无量纲潮汐形变率一致。热贡献随组分和转动状态不同而作用不同。摘要未给出更多数值、误差或显著性。

Conclusion: 作者认为所提出的演化方案可简单解释BNSM遗迹或CCSN形成PNS的长期演化，并补充无法覆盖完整演化时间尺度的动力学模拟。该工作面向中子星结构与演化，未涉及脉冲星单脉冲、模式转换、计时、偏振、射电频谱、磁星或FRB；对这些主题的关联根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: In this paper we compute several structural properties of static and rotating neutron stars, considering both rigid and differential rotation. To this end, we use a finite-temperature beta-stable equation of state (EOS) extending the zero-temperature hadronic EOS derived by Fortin et al. (2017). We account for the formation of hyperons and the presence of a neutrino-trapped component. Our goal is to provide insights into the long-term evolution of a protoneutron star (PNS) formed after a core-collapse supernova (CCSN) or a remnant resulting from a binary neutron star merger (BNSM). The EOS is derived using a relativistic mean-field quantum many-body theory within the Hartree approximation. Coupling constants are determined using experimental data on hypernuclei and hypernuclear matter to better account for uncertainties in hyperon single-particle potentials. Our EOS models reproduce the typical features of cold neutron stars and PNSs for nucleonic and hyperonic compositions. In particular, they are consistent with measured masses (2.01 M_sun and 2.14 M_sun for PSR J0348+0432 and PSR J0740+6620) and dimensionless tidal deformabilities from GW170817. Thermal contributions act differently depending on composition and rotation state. Finally, our evolutionary scheme provides a simple way to interpret the long-term evolution of BNSM remnants or PNSs from CCSNe, complementing dynamical simulations unable to cover full evolutionary timescales.

</details>

### [4] [Quantifying the coincidence between gravitational waves and fast radio bursts from neutron star-black hole mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.02534)
*Clarke, Teagan A.,Sarin, Nikhil,Howell, Eric J.,Lasky, Paul D.,Thrane, Eric*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该研究用模拟数据计算后验几率，以评估重合的中子星—黑洞（NSBH）并合与快速射电暴（FRB）是否来自共同源；结果表明，在至少两台观测设备的网络中并要求时间和天区位置重合时可获得统计显著的后验几率，但对于其模型，在 ln odds > 8 的阈值下需要网络信噪比大于10才能确信共同源探测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB的起源和机制仍不清楚，致密天体并合（包括NSBH并合）可能是产生某些FRB的通道，从而提供多信使引力波观测机会；但部分引力波信号可能是信噪比 < 8 的边际探测，或天区位置和距离不确定性较大，难以有把握地将FRB与引力波信号关联，因此需要避免假阳性关联。

Method: 摘要未说明具体的观测设备、频段和时间跨度；作者使用模拟数据来展示方法，并计算后验几率，即对重合的NSBH与FRB具有共同起源还是无关起源的相对置信度度量。

Result: 来自共同源的一个重合 FRB+NSBH 在一个至少有两台观测设备的网络中可产生统计显著的后验几率，但前提是要求时间和天区位置上的重合，而非仅要求时间重合；对于该模型，在采用 ln odds > 8 阈值时，需要网络信噪比大于10才能确信共同源探测。

Conclusion: 作者建议，一个重合的 NSBH+FRB 探测可能有助于通过区分瓦解模型和非瓦解模型来辨别FRB引擎。根据摘要无法判断该结论对其他模型或实际观测数据的适用性。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) are mysterious astrophysical transients whose origin and mechanism remain unclear. Compact object mergers may be a promising channel to produce some FRBs. Neutron star--black hole (NSBH) mergers could produce FRBs through mechanisms involving neutron star tidal disruption or magnetospheric disturbances. This could present an opportunity for multimessenger gravitational-wave observations, providing new insight into the nature of FRBs and nuclear matter. However, some of the gravitational-wave signals may be marginal detections with signal-to-noise ratios <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>&lt;</mml:mo><mml:mn>8</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula> or have large sky location and distance uncertainties, making it less straightforward to confidently associate an FRB with the gravitational-wave signal. One must therefore take care to avoid a false positive association. We demonstrate how to do this with simulated data. We calculate the posterior odds---a measurement of our relative belief for a common versus unrelated origin of a coincident NSBH and FRB. We find that a coincident <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>FRB</mml:mi><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mi>NSBH</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> from a common source can yield a statistically significant posterior odds in a network with at least two observatories, but only if we require a coincidence in time and sky location, rather than time alone. However, we find that, for our model, we require a network signal-to-noise ratio greater than 10 to be confident in the common-source detection, when using a threshold of ln odds <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>&gt;</mml:mo><mml:mn>8</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>. We suggest that a coincident <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>NSBH</mml:mi><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mi>FRB</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> detection could help distinguish between FRB engines by discriminating between disrupting and nondisrupting models.

</details>


### [5] [Fast Radio Bursts as Precursor Radio Emission from Monster Shocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.15076)
*Vanthieghem, A.,Levinson, A.*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 通过涵盖MHD尺度的粒子模拟，作者证实递减背景磁场中强磁化快磁声波陡化会形成 B^2-E^2→0 的怪物激波，耗散约一半波能量，并首次发现激波产生高频前兆波，其能量约占总耗散能量的 ~10^{-3}，在磁星条件下频率处于吉赫兹波段，可能表现为快速射电暴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期有人提出，强磁化中子星内磁层中MHD波的破裂可为不同类型高能暂现源供能。本文受此启发，研究强磁化快磁声波在递减背景磁场中的陡化与耗散，以检验怪物激波的形成及其是否可产生高频辐射。

Method: 摘要说明采用涵盖MHD尺度的粒子模拟（particle-in-cell simulations），研究在递减背景磁场中传播的强磁化快磁声波的陡化与耗散。摘要未说明具体磁场强度、时间跨度、样本或统计分析方法。

Result: 分析证实，当 B^2-E^2→0 时形成怪物激波，其耗散约一半的快磁声波能量。还首次揭示怪物激波产生高频前兆波，携带激波处耗散总能量的约 ~10^{-3}。前兆波频谱呈现若干尖锐谐波峰，在磁星预期条件下频率处于吉赫兹波段。

Conclusion: 作者认为，怪物激波产生的高频前兆波信号在磁星条件下可处于吉赫兹波段，此类信号可能表现为快速射电暴。根据摘要无法判断其与脉冲星单脉冲、模式转换、计时或自转减慢力矩变化之间是否存在联系。

Abstract: It has been proposed recently that the breaking of magnetohydrodynamics (MHD) waves in the inner magnetosphere of strongly magnetized neutron stars can power different types of high-energy transients. Motivated by these considerations, we study the steepening and dissipation of a strongly magnetized fast magnetosonic wave propagating in a declining background magnetic field, by means of particle-in-cell simulations that encompass MHD scales. Our analysis confirms the formation of a monster shock as <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>B</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>E</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mo>--&gt;</mml:mo><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, that dissipates about half of the fast magnetosonic wave energy. It also reveals, for the first time, the generation of a high-frequency precursor wave by the monster shock, carrying a fraction of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>3</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> of the total energy dissipated at the shock. The spectrum of the precursor wave exhibits several sharp harmonic peaks, with frequencies in the gigahertz band under conditions anticipated in magnetars. Such signals may appear as fast radio bursts.

</details>


### [6] [Gravitational lensing of fast radio bursts: Prospects for probing microlens populations in lensing galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.20305)
*Meena, Ashish Kumar,Saha, Prasenjit*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该工作研究不同微透镜族群在 \(|\mu|\)-\(t_d\) 平面上的分布，发现恒星质量族群与暗族群的自相关峰位于 \(>10^{-6}\) 秒和 \(<10^{-6}\) 秒的不同时间尺度，从而可能区分不同微透镜族群，并为未来用 FRB 微透镜约束恒星初始质量函数（IMF）提供途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质量 \(M\) 的恒星微透镜产生的像在天空中相隔微角秒、时间延迟约 \(2\times 10^{-5}(M/M_{\odot})\) 秒，无法在空间上分辨；但如果源是 FRB，则可能在时间上分辨微像。论文旨在研究不同微透镜族群导致的微像放大率与时间延迟分布，并评估利用 FRB 透镜效应探测强透镜星系内微透镜族群的可能性。

Method: 摘要中描述的研究内容为：研究由不同微透镜族群导致的微像的放大率（\(|\mu|\)）与时间延迟（\(t_d\)）分布，并分析自相关中的峰；讨论恒星密度变化对峰的影响，以及微像数目对 underlying 恒星质量函数的敏感性。摘要未说明具体观测、数据、望远镜或仪器、频段、时间跨度、样本和统计方法。

Result: 在星系透镜中典型强透镜（宏观）像的微透镜效应中，源自 \([0.08,1.5]M_{\odot}\) 范围恒星质量微透镜族群和位于 \([10^{-3}--10^{-2}]M_{\odot}\) 范围的第二（暗）微透镜族群的微像，位于 \(|\mu|\)-\(t_d\) 平面的不同部分。对于全局最小宏观像，由于恒星质量密度低，恒星族群导致自相关中 \(>10^{-6}\) 秒处的峰，而第二族群导致 \(<10^{-6}\) 秒处的峰。但恒星密度增加会引入 \(<10^{-6}\) 秒处的新峰，可能污染对多个微透镜族群存在的推断。微像数目也即自相关中峰数，对 underlying 恒星质量函数敏感。

Conclusion: 作者认为，这些差异允许区分不同微透镜族群，且微像数目对 underlying 恒星质量函数的敏感性使未来有可能用 FRB 微透镜约束恒星初始质量函数（IMF）。本工作是使用 FRB 透镜效应探测强透镜内微透镜族群的第一步；作者指出需要更详细的研究来评估仅定性讨论的各种不确定性的影响。

Abstract: Gravitational lensing by a stellar microlens of mass <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>M</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> forms two images separated by microarcseconds on the sky and has a time delay of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>5</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>/</mml:mo><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>M</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:mrow><mml:mo>)</mml:mo></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> seconds. Although we cannot resolve such microimages in the sky, they could be resolved in time if the source is a fast radio burst (FRB). In this work, we study the magnification (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>|</mml:mo><mml:mi>μ</mml:mi><mml:mo>|</mml:mo></mml:math></inline-formula>) and time delay (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>t</mml:mi><mml:mi>d</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>) distributions of microimages led by different microlens populations. We find that, in microlensing of typical strongly lensed (macro)images in galaxy lenses, microimages stemmed from a population of stellar mass microlenses in the <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mo>[</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.08</mml:mn><mml:mo>,</mml:mo><mml:mn>1.5</mml:mn><mml:mo>]</mml:mo><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>M</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:mrow></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> range and a second (dark) microlens population in <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mo>[</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>3</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mi>--</mml:mi><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mo>]</mml:mo><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>M</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:mrow></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> range reside in different parts of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>|</mml:mo><mml:mi>μ</mml:mi><mml:mo>|</mml:mo><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:msub><mml:mi>t</mml:mi><mml:mi>d</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> plane. For the global minimum macroimage, due to low stellar mass density, we find that the stellar population leads to peaks in autocorrelation at <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>&gt;</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>6</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> seconds, whereas the secondary population leads to peaks at <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>&lt;</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>6</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> seconds, allowing us to differentiate different microlens populations. However, an increase in stellar density introduces new peaks at <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>&lt;</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>6</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> seconds, which can pollute the inference about the presence of multiple microlens populations. In addition, we also show that the number of microimages, hence the number of peaks in the autocorrelation, is sensitive to the underlying stellar mass function, allowing us to constrain the stellar initial mass function (IMF) with FRB microlesning in the future. ### [7] [Phase transition and nuclear symmetry energy from neutron star observations: Constraints in light of PSR J0614-3329](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10025)
*Tang, Shao-Peng,Huang, Yong-Jia,Fan, Yi-Zhong*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该研究将 PSR J0614-3329 的最新半径测量纳入显式包含一阶强子—夸克相变的非参数高斯过程状态方程框架，得到微弱支持相变存在的贝叶斯因子 $B\approx2.3$，并对相变起始密度与对称能斜率参数 $L$ 给出了约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星内部是否发生一阶强子—夸克相变仍是未决问题，而改进的观测能否直接检验这种相变是关键挑战。该工作针对这一知识空白，引入包括 PSR J0614-3329 新半径测量在内的最新约束，以检验显式相变模型并约束相关状态方程与对称能参数。

Method: 将最新约束，尤其是中子星内部组成探索者（NICER）对 PSR J0614-3329 的新半径测量，纳入显式包含一阶相变的非参数高斯过程状态方程框架；通过比较包含与不包含显式相变的模型计算贝叶斯因子；并利用低密度（$<1.1n_s$）下的对称能展开推断斜率参数 $L$。其他观测细节、时间跨度与样本量摘要未说明。

Result: 比较包含与不包含显式相变的模型得到贝叶斯因子 $B\approx2.3$，微弱支持相变存在。在 68% 可信度下，相变起始密度 $n_{\mathrm{PT}}$ 要么低于 $2n_s$（对应质量 $\lesssim1M_\odot$，密度跳变 $\Delta n\sim0.5n_s$），要么更显著地高于 $4n_s$（接近最重中子星的中心密度，且 $\Delta n\sim3n_s$）。由低密度（$<1.1n_s$）对称能展开推断出 $L=40.2_{-14.3}^{+19.3}\ \mathrm{MeV}$，与核实验值吻合良好；$L$ 与 $1.4M_\odot$ 和 $2.0M_\odot$ 恒星之间的半径差呈正相关。

Conclusion: 作者认为，在显式包含一阶相变的非参数高斯过程状态方程框架下，最新约束（尤其 PSR J0614-3329 的新半径测量）对相变存在仅给出微弱支持，并给出两种可能的相变起始密度情形；同时，由对称能展开推断的 $L$ 与核实验值吻合良好，且 $L$ 与 $1.4M_\odot$ 和 $2.0M_\odot$ 恒星间的半径差正相关。该工作对脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲发射、快速射电暴等主题的直接联系，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: The possible occurrence of a first-order hadron-quark phase transition in neutron-star interiors remains an open question. Whether such a transition can be directly tested with improved observations is a key challenge. Here, we incorporate the latest constraints, especially a new radius measurement by the neutron star interior composition explorer for PSR J0614-3329, into a nonparametric Gaussian process equation of state framework that explicitly includes a first-order transition. We find a Bayes factor of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>B</mml:mi><mml:mo>≈</mml:mo><mml:mn>2.3</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula> when comparing models with and without an explicit phase transition, marginally favoring its presence. At 68% credibility, the transition onset density <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mi>PT</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> is either below <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>2</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mi>s</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> (corresponding to masses <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>, with density jump <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>∆</mml:mi><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.5</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mi>s</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>) or, more prominently, above <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>4</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mi>s</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> (near the central density of the heaviest neutron star, with <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>∆</mml:mi><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:mn>3</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mi>s</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>), where <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mi>s</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> represents the nuclear saturation density. In addition, by using symmetry-energy expansion at low densities (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>&lt;</mml:mo><mml:mn>1.1</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mi>s</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>), we infer a slope parameter <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>L</mml:mi><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:msubsup><mml:mn>40.2</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>14.3</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>19.3</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>MeV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>, in good agreement with nuclear-experiment values. Intriguingly, <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>L</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> correlates positively with the radius difference between <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>1.4</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>2.0</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> stars.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FRB已成为宇宙学中的有力探针，近期有优化方法通过最大化已定域FRB的河外色散量DM_ext联合似然函数来提取宇宙重子密度。该文关注的问题是：这一方法所用的DM_ext概率密度函数（PDF）中遗漏了一个关键因子，可能影响宇宙学推断的可靠性，因此需要识别并纠正该遗漏。

Method: 摘要未说明具体的观测仪器、频段或观测时间跨度。文中使用模拟的FRB数据来检验遗漏关键因子所造成的影响；随后将修正后的PDF应用于88个真实的已定域FRB样本，比较由原始PDF与修正PDF推断的重子密度，并与Planck 2018 CMB结果相对照；分析中采用最大化河外色散量DM_ext的联合似然函数。

Result: 摘要指出，在此前方法中DM_ext的概率密度函数遗漏了一个关键因子。使用模拟FRB数据时，忽略该因子会使推断的宇宙重子密度产生系统性偏差，偏差超过1σ置信水平。对88个真实已定域FRB样本，用原始PDF推断的重子密度与Planck 2018 CMB结果不一致，而修正PDF给出的结果与之一致性极好。

Conclusion: 作者认为，该缺失因子必须纳入DM_ext的概率密度函数，才能保证FRB在宇宙学应用中的可靠性；模拟结果显示忽略它会引起超过1σ的系统性偏差，而修正PDF对88个真实已定域FRB给出的重子密度与Planck 2018 CMB结果极好一致。关于该修正对脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲发射或FRB与磁星关联等方向的可迁移性，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Fast radio bursts (FRBs) have emerged as powerful probes in cosmology. An optimized method was recently proposed to extract the cosmic baryon density from localized FRBs by maximizing the joint likelihood function of the extragalactic dispersion measure (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>DM</mml:mi><mml:mi>ext</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>). In this paper, we identify a crucial factor that was omitted in the probability density function (PDF) for <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>DM</mml:mi><mml:mi>ext</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> in that method. Using simulated FRB data, we demonstrate that neglecting this factor leads to a systematic bias in the inferred cosmic baryon density, with deviations exceeding the <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:mi>σ</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> confidence level. This highlights the necessity of including the missing factor for reliable cosmological applications of FRBs. Furthermore, applying our corrected PDF to a sample of 88 real localized FRBs, we find that the baryon density inferred with the original PDF is inconsistent with the Planck 2018 CMB results, whereas our corrected PDF yields excellent agreement.

TL;DR: 该文通过含超核物质到色超导夸克物质相变的混合状态方程研究旋转混合星，表明在强矢量耦合的最有利参数组下，带色超导夸克物质核心的混合星构型可以描述最快自转且最重的银河系中子星 PSR J0952-0607，而纯强子超核星构型无法做到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中子星高自转性质与致密物质状态方程密切相关，尤其是退禁闭相变是否影响高自转混合星的性质尚需研究。此前关于开普勒频率、引力质量与非自转中子星半径的经验关系建立在所有中子星直至最重者均为强子星的假设之上，该假设是否成立以及相变如何影响对致密物质状态方程的约束，是本文关注的问题。

Method: 采用相对论密度泛函方法描述超核物质到色超导夸克物质的相变，构建混合状态方程；通过改变夸克物质相中的矢量介子耦合和双夸克耦合，得到具有不同夸克物质核心延伸尺度的混合星序列，并使其与质量、半径和潮汐形变率测量的天体物理约束保持一致。摘要未说明具体的观测数据、仪器、频段、时间跨度或样本。

Conclusion: 作者认为，向夸克物质的相变会改变开普勒频率、引力质量与非自转中子星半径之间的经验关系，从而改变对致密物质状态方程的约束；引入混合状态方程对强相互作用物质和中子星性质的约束具有重要影响，并由此给出上述半径上限。根据摘要无法判断该结果对脉冲星计时、单脉冲发射、模式转换、偏振或磁星与快速射电暴（FRB）联系的具体可迁移性。

Abstract: We study rotating hybrid stars, with a particular emphasis on the effect of a deconfinement phase transition on their properties at high spin. Our analysis is based on a hybrid equation of state with a phase transition from hypernuclear matter to color-superconducting quark matter, where both phases are described within a relativistic density functional approach. By varying the vector meson and diquark couplings in the quark matter phase, we obtain different hybrid star sequences with varying extension of the quark matter core, ensuring consistency with astrophysical constraints from mass, radius, and tidal deformability measurements. As a result, we demonstrate the impact of an increasing rotational frequency on the maximum gravitational mass, the central energy density of compact stars, the appearance of the quasiradial oscillations, and nonaxisymmetric instabilities. We demonstrate that for the most favorable parameter sets with a strong vector coupling, hybrid star configurations with a color superconducting quark matter core can describe the fastest spinning and heaviest galactic neutron star PSR J0952-0607, while it is out of reach for the purely hadronic hypernuclear star configuration. We also revise the previously proposed empirical relation between the Kepler frequency, gravitational mass, and radius of nonrotating neutron stars, obtained based on the assumption that all neutron stars, up to the heaviest, are hadronic. We show how the phase transition to quark matter alters this relation and, consequently, the constraints on the dense matter equation of state. Our findings reveal that incorporating the hybrid equation of state has significant implications for the constraints on the properties of strongly interacting matter and neutron stars, placing the upper limit on <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>R</mml:mi><mml:mn>1.4</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>&lt;=</mml:mo><mml:mn>14.90</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>R</mml:mi><mml:mn>0.7</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>&lt;</mml:mo><mml:mn>11.49</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>km</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> (considering 716 Hz frequency limit from PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>1748</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>2446</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mi>ad</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>) and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>R</mml:mi><mml:mn>1.4</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>&lt;=</mml:mo><mml:mn>11.90</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>km</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> (for 1000 Hz).

TL;DR: 该文利用地面核物理数据、NICER 半径与 GW170817 潮汐形变度对中子星状态方程进行贝叶斯约束与模型比较，在全部数据合并情形下模型比较倾向于 Skyrme 模型，并给出 $L_0=56\pm3\ \mathrm{MeV}$、$K_{\mathrm{sym}0}=-132\pm15\ \mathrm{MeV}$、$R_{1.4}=11.85\pm0.11\ \mathrm{km}$ 等紧致约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在中子星状态方程研究中，对称核物质与对称能参数以及中子星半径、潮汐形变度各自受到不同数据和模型假设的限制；摘要指出需要综合地面核物理输入、NICER 半径与 GW170817 数据，并在多种 EoS 模型之间进行贝叶斯比较，以获得对状态方程参数更为紧致的约束。

Method: 摘要说明使用地面与天体物理数据开展综合贝叶斯分析：地面输入为由有限核和重离子碰撞导出的对称核物质与对称能量，天体物理约束为近期 NICER 观测半径与 GW170817 潮汐形变度；考虑 Taylor、$n/3$、Skyrme、相对平均场和声速（CS）五种 EoS 模型；按 (i) 至 (v) 五种顺序更新先验，并进行贝叶斯模型比较。具体统计方法细节摘要未说明。

Result: 在所有数据合并的情形 (iv) 下，贝叶斯模型比较倾向于 Skyrme 模型，并给出对称能参数 $L_0=56\pm3\ \mathrm{MeV}$、$K_{\mathrm{sym}0}=-132\pm15\ \mathrm{MeV}$，以及 SNM 参数 $K_0=265\pm12\ \mathrm{MeV}$、$Q_0=-366\pm43\ \mathrm{MeV}$。质量-半径和质量--潮汐形变度后验分布得到很好约束；$1.4M_\odot$ 中子星的半径与潮汐形变度分别为 $R_{1.4}=11.85\pm0.11\ \mathrm{km}$ 和 $\Lambda_{1.4}=354\pm25$。

Conclusion: 作者依据摘要所述结果得出，在合并全部数据时贝叶斯模型比较支持 Skyrme 模型，并由此对对称能与对称核物质参数、质量-半径及质量--潮汐形变度后验分布给出紧致约束，得到特定的 $1.4M_\odot$ 中子星半径与潮汐形变度。该文属于中子星状态方程与多物理数据约束工作；根据摘要无法判断其与脉冲星单脉冲、脉冲轮廓演化、模式转换、计时、射电偏振、射电频谱或磁星—FRB 联系的具体关系。

Abstract: We perform a comprehensive Bayesian analysis to constrain the neutron star (NS) equation of state (EoS) using a wide range of terrestrial and astrophysical data. The terrestrial inputs include quantities related to symmetric nuclear matter (SNM) and symmetry energy up to two times saturation density (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>ρ</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>≃</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.16</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>fm</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>3</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>), derived from finite nuclei and heavy ion collisions (HIC). The astrophysical constraints incorporate NS radii and tidal deformabilities from recent Neutron Star Interior Composition Explorer (NICER) observations and GW170817, respectively. We consider five different EoS models: Taylor, <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mo>/</mml:mo><mml:mn>3</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>, Skyrme, relative mean field, and sound speed (CS), and analyze them by sequentially updating the priors with (i) chiral effective field theory (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>χ</mml:mi><mml:mi>EFT</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>)-based pure neutron matter, (ii) terrestrial, empirical, and earlier astrophysical data, (iii) case (ii) including NICER radii of PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>0437</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>4715</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>0614</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>3329</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, (iv) all data combined, and (v) excluding empirical nuclear inputs. We also perform Bayesian model comparison, which favors the Skyrme model under all combined data [scenario (iv)], yielding tight constraints on symmetry energy parameters: <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>L</mml:mi><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>56</mml:mn><mml:mo>±</mml:mo><mml:mn>3</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>MeV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>K</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mi>sym</mml:mi><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>132</mml:mn><mml:mo>±</mml:mo><mml:mn>15</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>MeV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>, and also on SNM parameters: <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>K</mml:mi><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>265</mml:mn><mml:mo>±</mml:mo><mml:mn>12</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>MeV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>Q</mml:mi><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>366</mml:mn><mml:mo>±</mml:mo><mml:mn>43</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>MeV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>. The mass-radius and mass--tidal deformability posterior distributions are also well constrained. The radius and tidal deformability of a <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>1.4</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> neutron star are found to be <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>R</mml:mi><mml:mn>1.4</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>11.85</mml:mn><mml:mo>±</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.11</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>km</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>Λ</mml:mi><mml:mn>1.4</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>354</mml:mn><mml:mo>±</mml:mo><mml:mn>25</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>, respectively.

</details>

TL;DR: 该研究考察了与QCD耦合的极轻轴子模型中形成的轴子凝聚中子星，并利用吸积中子星热弛豫、孤立中子星冷却和脉冲星glitch的观测，在 $\theta=\pi$ 的轴子凝聚相下限制极轻轴子参数空间，排除了在 $m_a\gtrsim 2\times 10^{-9}\ \mathrm{eV}$ 时直到 $5\times 10^{-7}\lesssim\epsilon\lesssim 0.2$ 的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极轻轴子可解决强CP问题，但其质量平方比QCD贡献轻 $\epsilon<1$ 倍；在致密物质中轴子势改变，中子星大部分物质可处于 $\theta=\pi$ 的轴子凝聚相，从而改变核子质量与相互作用，进而影响状态方程、结构和现象学。摘要在该背景下研究这一凝聚相对中子星物质EOS及轴子参数空间限制的影响。

Method: 摘要说明其在手征有效场论框架内首次研究 $\theta=\pi$ 处中子星物质的EOS，并使用相对论平均场论推断核物质和中子星低密度EOS的变化；同时基于吸积中子星热弛豫、孤立中子星冷却和脉冲星glitch的观测推导对极轻轴子参数空间的限制。摘要未说明具体望远镜、频段、样本、时间跨度及统计方法。

Result: 摘要报告在致密物质中轴子势改变， $\theta=\pi$ 的轴子凝聚相占据中子星大部分物质且CP保持良好对称性，核子质量与相互作用被修改，从而改变EOS、结构和现象学；并基于吸积中子星热弛豫、孤立中子星冷却和脉冲星glitch观测，排除在 $m_a\gtrsim 2\times 10^{-9}\ \mathrm{eV}$ 时直到 $5\times 10^{-7}\lesssim\epsilon\lesssim 0.2$ 的参数区域。

Conclusion: 作者推导了对极轻轴子参数空间的限制，并评论了对中子星质量-半径关系的潜在改变，还讨论了具有 $\theta=\pi$ 的新型核密度致密天体的可能性，这些天体不是由引力而是由轴子势稳定。摘要未给出质量-半径具体变化量。与脉冲星glitch的关联仅体现为所用观测之一；关于磁星、快速射电暴或单脉冲发射的联系，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive study of axion condensed neutron stars that arise in models of an exceptionally light axion that couples to quantum chromodynamics (QCD). These axions solve the strong-charge-parity (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>C</mml:mi><mml:mi>P</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>) problem, but have a mass-squared lighter than that due to QCD by a factor of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>ϵ</mml:mi><mml:mo>&lt;</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>. Inside dense matter, the axion potential is altered, and much of the matter in neutron stars resides in the axion condensed phase where the strong-<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>C</mml:mi><mml:mi>P</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> parameter <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>θ</mml:mi><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mi>π</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>C</mml:mi><mml:mi>P</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> remains a good symmetry. In these regions, masses and interactions of nucleons are modified, in turn changing the equation of state (EOS), structure, and phenomenology of the neutron stars. We take the first steps toward the study of the EOS of neutron star matter at <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>θ</mml:mi><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mi>π</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> within chiral effective field theory and use relativistic mean field theory to deduce the resulting changes to nuclear matter and the neutron star low-density EOS. We derive constraints on the exceptionally light axion parameter space based on observations of the thermal relaxation of accreting neutron stars, isolated neutron star cooling, and pulsar glitches, excluding the region up to <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>5</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>7</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:mi>ϵ</mml:mi><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> for <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>m</mml:mi><mml:mi>a</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>≳</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>9</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>eV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>. We comment on potential changes to the neutron star mass-radius relationship, and discuss the possibility of novel, nuclear-density compact objects with <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>θ</mml:mi><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mi>π</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> that are stabilized not by gravity but by the axion potential.

TL;DR: 本文建立了磁化正负电子对等离子体中电磁波参量不稳定性的理论框架，首次解析推导了低于回旋频率的诱导康普顿散射线性增长率；将其应用于快速射电暴时，所识别的回旋半径效应与德拜屏蔽效应可促进X模波从磁星和中子星磁层及外流中逃逸，从而实现观测到的100%偏振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁化正负电子对等离子体中电磁波的非线性相互作用此前缺乏一致的理论描述，尤其是诱导康普顿散射在磁化、频率低于回旋频率情形下的线性增长率尚未得到解析推导。本文旨在建立该参量不稳定性的理论框架，并用于理解快速射电暴的偏振逃逸问题。

Method: 摘要说明为理论解析工作：针对磁化正负电子对等离子体中的电磁波，建立参量不稳定性的理论表述，并解析推导频率低于回旋频率时诱导康普顿散射的线性增长率；进而分析密度涨落模式以及垂直磁场偏振波所受的抑制效应。具体观测数据、仪器、频段、样本与统计方法摘要未说明。

Result: 摘要报告：首次解析给出频率低于回旋频率时的诱导康普顿散射线性增长率；识别出寻常、带电和中性三种密度涨落模式；在带电模式中有质动力沿纵向分离电子与正电子，与非磁化情形不同；并识别出两种显著降低垂直磁场偏振波散射率的效应，即磁场抑制粒子轨道导致的回旋半径效应，以及对大于德拜波长的波长起作用的德拜屏蔽效应。摘要未给出具体数值。

Conclusion: 作者将上述结果应用于快速射电暴，认为这些效应促进X模波从磁星和中子星的磁层及外流中逃逸，从而实现观测到的100%偏振；并指出该表述为天体物理和激光物理中一致处理电磁波与磁化等离子体非线性相互作用提供了基础。该框架对脉冲星、磁星计时及单脉冲等具体观测的可迁移性，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: A formulation for the parametric instability of electromagnetic waves in magnetized pair plasma is developed. The linear growth rate of induced Compton scattering is derived analytically for frequencies below the cyclotron frequency for the first time. We identify three modes of density fluctuation: ordinary, charged, and neutral modes. In the charged mode, the ponderomotive force separates charges (electrons and positrons) longitudinally, in contrast to the nonmagnetized case. We also recognize two effects that significantly reduce the scattering rate for waves polarized perpendicular to the magnetic field: (1) the gyroradius effect due to the magnetic suppression of particle orbits and (2) Debye screening effect for wavelengths larger than the Debye length. Applying this to fast radio bursts, we find that these effects facilitate the escape of X-mode waves from the magnetosphere and outflow of a magnetar and neutron star, enabling 100% polarization as observed. Our formulation provides a foundation for consistently addressing the nonlinear interaction of electromagnetic waves with magnetized plasma in astrophysics and laser physics.

Result: 摘要报告，中子星磁层中的等离子体可通过共振转换效应显著增强偏振。基于 PSR B0656+14、4U 0142+61 的偏振测量以及中红外波段的基准偏振测量，作者证明中子星的光学和红外偏振能够在宽广的轴子质量范围 $10^{-11} \lesssim m_a \lesssim 10^{-3}\ \mathrm{eV}$ 内对轴子-光子耦合给出强限制。

Conclusion: 作者明确支持的结论是：中子星磁层等离子体通过共振转换效应显著增强偏振，因此中子星的光学和红外偏振测量可作为探测轴子-光子耦合的强有力手段，并在 $10^{-11} \lesssim m_a \lesssim 10^{-3}\ \mathrm{eV}$ 的质量范围内给出强限制。该结果与脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲发射、脉冲轮廓演化、模式转换、计时、偏振或 FRB 的直接物理联系：根据摘要无法判断；在方法层面，偏振测量作为限制新物理参数的方法或可具有谨慎的参考意义，但摘要并未将其作为这些主题的结果加以确立。

Abstract: Neutron stars provide ideal astrophysical laboratories for probing new physics beyond the Standard Model. If axions exist, then photons can develop linear polarization during photon-axion conversion in the magnetic field of a neutron star. We find that the plasma in the neutron star magnetosphere could dramatically enhance the polarization through the resonant conversion effect. With the polarization measurements from PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>B</mml:mi><mml:mn>0656</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>14</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, 4U <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>0142</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>61</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, and the benchmark polarization measurement in the midinfrared band, we demonstrate that optical and infrared polarization from neutron stars can provide strong constraints on the axion-photon coupling over a broad axion mass range <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>11</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:msub><mml:mi>m</mml:mi><mml:mi>a</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>3</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>eV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>.

Abstract: Driven by recent laboratory experiments and astronomical observations, significant advances have deepened our understanding of neutron-star physics. NICER's Pulse Profile Modeling has refined our knowledge of neutron star masses and radii, while gravitational-wave detections have revealed key insights into the structure of neutron stars. Particularly relevant is the extraction of the tidal deformability by the LIGO-Virgo collaboration and the most recent determination of stellar radii by NICER, both suggesting a relatively soft equation of state (EOS) at intermediate densities. Additionally, measurements from the PREX collaboration and from pulsar timing suggest instead that the EOS is stiff in the vicinity of saturation density and at the highest densities accessible to date. But how stiff can the EOS be at these very high densities? Recent events featuring compact objects near the ``lower mass gap'' have raised questions about the existence of very massive neutron stars. Motivated by this finding and in light of new refinements to theoretical models, we explore the possibility that these massive objects may indeed be rapidly rotating neutron stars. We explore how rotation affects both the maximum neutron star mass and their associated radii, and discuss the implications they may have on the equation of state.

This study uses two large sets of relativistic mean-field equations of state with either nucleonic or nucleonic and hyperonic degrees of freedom, and imposes constraints obtained from GW170817 and the pulsars PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>0030</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0451</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> and PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>0740</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>6620</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>. It is shown that: (i) some mass-radius curves are characterized by a negative slope from one solar mass up to the maximum mass; (ii) other equations of state (EoSs) have a positive slope for a given range of masses below the maximum star mass. Within the set of models considered, the first set includes only a very small number of hyperonic EoSs: fewer than 0.5% of the total number of hyperonic stars and approximately one third of the nucleonic EoS. We have also analyzed the sign of the slope for neutron star masses of 1.2, 1.4 and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>1.8</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>. Only approximately 1% of hyperonic EoSs predict a negative slope for <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>1.4</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> stars, whereas over 90% of nucleonic stars have a negative slope at this mass. Finally, almost all stars have a negative slope at <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>1.8</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>. A positive slope at <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>1.4</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> may indicate the presence of non-nucleonic degrees of freedom within neutron stars. The nuclear matter property that distinguishes the different scenarios most clearly is the curvature of the symmetry energy. Nucleonic EoSs with a positive slope <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>d</mml:mi><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>/</mml:mo><mml:mi>d</mml:mi><mml:mi>R</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> predict the highest values, which can exceed 100 MeV.

Abstract: Millisecond pulsars, representing the older neutron star population, are believed to have undergone a prolonged period of dark matter accumulation, resulting in a higher dark matter content. We examine the impact of dark matter rotation on the macroscopic properties of neutron stars, including the mass-radius relation, the mass-shedding Keplerian limit, and moments of inertia, for various dark matter particle masses and total fractions, considering both core and halo distributions. Abstract: Pulsars are rotating neutron stars that are observed to be slowing down, implying a loss of their rotational energy. There can be several different physical mechanisms involved in their spin-down process. In this work, we examine three different physical phenomena contributing to the spin-down: magnetic dipole radiation, gravitational mass quadrupole radiation due to the ``mountain'' formation, and gravitational mass current quadrupole radiation---or the r-modes---and calculate the expressions for the braking indices due to all of them. In all cases, the rapid rotation results in a departure of the braking index from the standard values when the rotational effects are ignored. If generated with a saturation amplitude within the range of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>4</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mi>--</mml:mi><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, the r-mode oscillations dominate the spin-down of millisecond pulsars. Moreover, we explore the braking index in the context of millisecond magnetars. We also study the effects of different choices of baryon mass on the braking indices.

By systematically varying the chiral invariant mass <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>m</mml:mi><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> and quark matter parameters, we determine which values simultaneously satisfy all current astrophysical constraints, including gravitational wave observations from GW170817, NICER measurements of several pulsars, and the existence of two-solar-mass neutron stars. The inclusion of PSR J0614-3329 dramatically refines the allowed range of the chiral invariant mass from the previous constraint of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>580</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>MeV</mml:mi><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:msub><mml:mi>m</mml:mi><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:mn>860</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>MeV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> to <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>800</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>MeV</mml:mi><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:msub><mml:mi>m</mml:mi><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:mn>860</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>MeV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>, raising the lower bound by approximately 220 MeV. Abstract: We studied the properties of dark matter admixed-neutron stars (DMANS), considering fermionic dark matter (DM) that interacts gravitationally with hadronic matter (HM). Using relativistic mean-field equations of state (EOSs) for both components, we solved the two-fluid Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff equations to determine neutron star (NS) properties assuming that DM is confined within the stellar core. For hadronic matter, we employed realistic EOSs derived from low energy nuclear physics experiments, heavy-ion collision data, and NS observations. To constrain key dark matter parameters---such as particle mass, mass fraction, and the coupling to mass ratio---we applied Bayesian inference, incorporating various astrophysical data including mass, radii, and NICER mass-radius distributions for PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>0740</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>6620</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> and PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>0030</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0451</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>. Additionally, we explored the influence of high-density HM EOSs and examined the impact of stiffer hadronic EOSs, excluding the vector meson self-interaction term. Our findings indicate that current astrophysical observations primarily constrain the dark matter fraction, while providing limited constraints on the particle mass or coupling. However, the dark matter fraction is largely insensitive to how astrophysical observations or uncertainties in the high-density EOS are incorporated. Instead, it is predominantly determined by the stiffness of the hadronic EOS at high densities, with stiffer hadronic EOSs yielding a higher dark matter mass fraction. Therefore, we conclude that the dark matter fraction plays a crucial role in shaping the properties of DMANS. Future investigations incorporating more realistic EOSs and astrophysical observations of other compact objects may provide deeper insights into dark matter.

Abstract: Numerical simulations are essential to understand the complex physics accompanying the merger of binary systems of neutron stars. However, these simulations become computationally challenging when they have to model the merger remnants on timescales over which secular phenomena, such as the launching of magnetically driven outflows, develop. To tackle these challenges, we have recently developed a hybrid approach that combines, via a handoff transition, a fully general-relativistic code (FIL) with a more efficient code making use of the conformally flat approximation (BHAC+). We report here important additional developments of BHAC+ consisting of the inclusion of gravitational-wave radiation-reaction contributions and of higher-order formulations of the equations of general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamics. Both improvements have allowed us to explore binary neutron-star merger remnants with high accuracy and over timescales that would have been computationally prohibitive otherwise. More specifically, we have investigated the impact of the magnetic-field strength on the long-term (i.e., <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:mn>200</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>ms</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>) and high-resolution (i.e., 150 m) evolutions of the ``magnetar'' resulting from the merger of two neutron stars with a realistic equation of state. In this way, and for sufficiently large magnetic fields, we observe the weakening or suppression of differential rotation and the generation of magnetic flares in the outer layers of the remnant. These flares, driven mostly by the Parker instability, are responsible for intense and collimated Poynting flux outbursts and mass ejections. This novel phenomenology offers the possibility of seeking corresponding signatures from the observations of short gamma-ray bursts and hence revealing the existence of a long-lived strongly magnetized remnant.

Abstract: The implications of perturbative QCD (pQCD) calculations on neutron stars are carefully examined. While pQCD calculations above baryon chemical potentials <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mi>μ</mml:mi><mml:mi>B</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>≈</mml:mo><mml:mn>2.4</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> GeV demonstrate the potential of ruling out a wide range of neutron star equations of state (EOSs), such constraints only affect the most massive neutron stars in the vicinity of the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff limit, resulting in constraints that are orthogonal to current or expected astrophysical bounds. In the most constraining scenario, pQCD considerations favor low values of the squared speed sound <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>C</mml:mi><mml:mi>s</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> at high <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>μ</mml:mi><mml:mi>B</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> relevant for the most massive neutron stars, but leave predictions of the radii and tidal deformabilities almost unchanged. Such considerations become irrelevant if the maximum speed of sound squared inside neutron stars does not exceed about <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mi>C</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mi>s</mml:mi><mml:mo>,</mml:mo><mml:mi>max</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>≈</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.5</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, or if pQCD breaks down below <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mi>μ</mml:mi><mml:mi>B</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>≈</mml:mo><mml:mn>2.9</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> GeV. Furthermore, the large pQCD uncertainties preclude any meaningful bounds on the neutron star EOS at the moment. Interestingly, if pQCD predictions for the pressure at around <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mi>μ</mml:mi><mml:mi>B</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>≈</mml:mo><mml:mn>2.5</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> GeV are refined and found to be low (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:mn>1.5</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> GeV/<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>fm</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mn>3</mml:mn></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula>), evidence for a soft neutron star inner core EOS in combination with the existence of two-solar-mass pulsars would indicate the presence of color superconductivity beyond neutron star densities. I point out that two-solar-mass pulsars place robust upper bounds on this nonperturbative effect and require the pairing gap to be less than <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mi>∆</mml:mi><mml:mi>CFL</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>&lt;=</mml:mo><mml:mn>500</mml:mn><mml:mspace></mml:mspace><mml:mtext>MeV</mml:mtext></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> at <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mi>μ</mml:mi><mml:mi>B</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>≈</mml:mo><mml:mn>2.5</mml:mn><mml:mspace></mml:mspace><mml:mtext>GeV</mml:mtext></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>.

We develop a Bayesian framework and forecast that third-generation GW detectors are required to obtain meaningful constraints. The method can also be used with luminosity distances obtained through other means than GWs.

Abstract: We investigate the structural and observable impacts of dark matter (DM) on neutron stars (NSs) using a combined equation of state that integrates the relativistic mean-field (RMF) model for baryonic matter with a variable density profile for DM, incorporating DM-baryon interactions mediated by the Higgs field. This origin further indicates that the multibranch structure emerges for both negative and positive <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>β</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>, the quadratic-term coefficient in the effective coupling function between the scalar field and conventional matter in the Einstein frame. By comparing the Damour--Esposito-Farèse and Mendes-Ortiz models of the ST gravity, we demonstrate that their distinct scalarization behaviors stem from whether the effective coupling function is bounded. We also compute for scalarized NSs with a highly relativistic dense core in ST theories the moment of inertia and tidal deformability that are relevant to pulsar-timing and gravitational-wave experiments.

Abstract: The equation of state of dense matter remains a central challenge in astrophysics and high-energy physics, particularly at supranuclear densities where exotic degrees of freedom like hyperons or deconfined quarks are expected to appear. The model extends the density-dependent formalism by introducing a Polyakov-loop-inspired scalar field to dynamically govern the hadron-quark phase transition, following the approach of chiral mean-field models. The resulting model is consistent with a wide range of theoretical and observational constraints, including those from chiral effective field theory, massive pulsars, gravitational-wave events, and NICER data. We analyze its thermodynamic properties by constructing the QCD phase diagram, identifying the deconfinement, chiral, and nuclear liquid-gas transitions. As a first application, we model the evolution of protoneutron stars using isentropic snapshots and explore the implications of the slow stable hybrid star hypothesis. Our findings establish EVA--01 as a robust and versatile framework for exploring dense matter, bridging the gap between microphysical models and multimessenger astrophysical observations.

We could also constrain the critical temperature for neutron superfluidity in the neutron-star core considering that the former four magnetars are probably precessing, and the most stringent constraint is <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>T</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>c</mml:mi><mml:mo>,</mml:mo><mml:mi>core</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>&lt;</mml:mo><mml:mn>6.4</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>8</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>K</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>. In contrast to other explanations based on boson stars, our <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>SS</mml:mi><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mi>MDM</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> model offers a natural explanation for this system. We provide detailed mass-radius comparisons with observational data and discuss future observations that could test the predictions of our model, offering new insights into neutron star structure and the role of dark matter in compact objects.

TL;DR: 该工作利用 EPTA 的脉冲星偏振测量数据搜寻超轻类轴子暗物质：频率学派分析未发现显著信号，贝叶斯分析在多个脉冲星中发现共同频率 \(10^{-8}\)–\(2\times10^{-8}\ \mathrm{Hz}\) 的显著信号，但最可能源于地球电离层残余法拉第旋转，并在 \(6\times10^{-24}\)–\(5\times10^{-21}\ \mathrm{eV}\) 质量范围给出对耦合常数 \(g_{a\gamma}\) 的强限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻类轴子粒子（ALPs）可作为暗物质的可行候选；与 ALPs 相关的标量场与电磁场耦合后充当主动双折射介质，可通过轴子场振荡诱导天体物理信号线偏振平面的单色变化。毫秒脉冲星射电辐射具有高线偏振度和可忽略的偏振性质涨落，因而是搜寻此类表现的优良工具。

Method: 使用为欧洲脉冲星计时阵列（EPTA）campaign 收集并预处理的脉冲星偏振测量数据，聚焦线偏振最亮的十二个源，采用频率学派与贝叶斯两种统计框架搜寻轴子的天体物理信号。频率学派分析以 Lomb-Scargle 周期图为核心；贝叶斯分析所用模型经调整以容纳数据中可能存在的多种确定性系统误差。观测频率、时间跨度等细节摘要未说明。

Result: 频率学派分析未发现统计上显著的信号。贝叶斯分析在多个脉冲星数据集中发现了一个统计上显著的信号，其共同频率介于 \(10^{-8}\) 与 \(2\times10^{-8}\ \mathrm{Hz}\) 之间，最可能由地球电离层的残余法拉第旋转解释。对耦合常数 \(g_{a\gamma}\) 获得了强限制，与其他搜寻处于同一量级，限制覆盖的质量范围为 \(6\times10^{-24}\) 至 \(5\times10^{-21}\ \mathrm{eV}\)。

Conclusion: 作者在 \(6\times10^{-24}\) 至 \(5\times10^{-21}\ \mathrm{eV}\) 质量范围内给出了对耦合常数 \(g_{a\gamma}\) 的强限制，并指出多个脉冲星中共同频率 \(10^{-8}\)–\(2\times10^{-8}\ \mathrm{Hz}\) 的显著信号最可能由地球电离层残余法拉第旋转解释而非轴子。作者还讨论了限制脉冲星偏振测量搜寻超轻轴子灵敏度的若干问题及可能解决途径。该工作对磁星、快速射电暴或单脉冲发射的物理联系，摘要未说明；对脉冲星偏振测量方法论的可迁移性，摘要亦未说明。

Abstract: Ultralight axionlike particles (ALPs) can be a viable solution to the dark matter problem. The scalar field associated with ALPs, coupled to the electromagnetic field acts as an active birefringent medium, altering the polarization properties of light through which it propagates. In particular, oscillations of the axionic field induce monochromatic variations of the plane of linearly polarized radiation of astrophysical signals. The radio emission of millisecond pulsars provides an excellent tool to search for such manifestations, given their high fractional linear polarization and negligible fluctuations of their polarization properties. We have searched for evidence of ALPs in polarimetry measurements of pulsars collected and preprocessed for the European Pulsar Timing Array (EPTA) campaign. Focusing on the twelve brightest sources in linear polarization, we searched for an astrophysical signal from axions using both frequentist and Bayesian statistical frameworks. For the frequentist analysis, which uses Lomb-Scargle periodograms at its core, no statistically significant signal has been found. The model used for the Bayesian analysis has been adjusted to accommodate multiple deterministic systematics that may be present in the data. A statistically significant signal has been found in the dataset of multiple pulsars with common frequencies between <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>8</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>8</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>Hz</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, which can most likely be explained by the residual Faraday rotation in the terrestrial ionosphere. Strong bounds on the coupling constant <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>g</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mi>a</mml:mi><mml:mi>γ</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>, in the same ballpark as other searches, have been obtained in the mass range between <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>6</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>24</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>5</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>21</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>eV</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>. We conclude by discussing the problems that can limit the sensitivity of our search for ultralight axions in the polarimetry data of pulsars, and possible ways to resolve them.

</details>

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 在广义相对论磁流体动力学模拟中，磁场通过磁制动和磁有效湍流黏性使双中子星并合遗迹趋向均匀转动，导致主导并合后引力波模式的 f_2 频率随时间上移，最强磁场情形下总体移动可达 ~200 Hz，中位情形为 ~50 Hz。作者还表明极大的磁场或显著切变黏性可使超大质量中子星遗迹在不到100 ms内坍缩为黑洞并形成喷流，但认为这种结果的条件并不现实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前利用数值相对论模拟导出的并合后关键振荡频率准普适关系大多忽略磁场，而磁场可能影响并合遗迹转动、角动量损失以及并合后引力波振荡频率，因此需要检验磁场强度与拓扑如何改变这些关系，并为未来引力波观测分析提供依据。

Method: 开展双中子星并合的完整广义相对论磁流体动力学模拟；采用两种不同质量、两种EOS（SLy和ALF2）、三种初始磁场拓扑（仅极向和仅环向、局限于内部，以及“脉冲星式”偶极极向延伸至外部）、四种磁场强度，插入时刻最大磁场值从 5.5×10^15 到 2.2×10^17 G；分析并合后引力波振荡频率与并合后电磁辐射。其他细节摘要未说明。

Result: 磁制动和磁有效湍流黏性驱动并合遗迹趋向均匀转动，并增加总体角动量损失；主导并合后引力波模式的 f_2 频率随时间向上移动，最强磁场情形总体移动可达 ~200 Hz，中位情形为 ~50 Hz，因此原则上可被未来引力波天文台探测到。作者还发现，极大磁场或显著切变黏性机制可使超大质量中子星遗迹在不到100 ms内坍缩为黑洞并导致喷流形成。

Conclusion: 作者认为，未来引力波天文台的分析应包含磁场，因为磁场引起的 f_2 频率上移原则上可被探测；同时，极大磁场或显著切变黏性可导致超大质量中子星遗迹快速坍缩并形成喷流，但作者并不预期这种结果的条件是现实的。关于脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、模式转换、极化、射电频谱或FRB的联系，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: The oscillation modes of neutron star (NS) merger remnants, as encoded by the kHz postmerger gravitational wave (GW) signal, hold great potential for constraining the as-yet undetermined equation of state (EOS) of dense nuclear matter. Previous works have used numerical relativity simulations to derive quasiuniversal relations for the key oscillation frequencies, but most of them omit the effects of a magnetic field. We conduct full general-relativistic magnetohydrodynamics simulations of NSNS mergers with two different masses and two different EOSs (SLy and ALF2) with three different initial magnetic field topologies (poloidal and toroidal only, confined to the interior, and ``pulsarlike'': dipolar poloidal extending from the interior to the exterior), with four different magnetic field strengths with maximum values ranging from <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>5.5</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mn>15</mml:mn></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> to <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>2.2</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mn>17</mml:mn></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>G</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> at the time of insertion. We find that magnetic braking and magnetic effective turbulent viscosity drives the merger remnants towards uniform rotation and increases their overall angular momentum loss. As a result, the <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>f</mml:mi><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> frequency of the dominant postmerger GW mode shifts upwards over time. The overall shift is up to <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:mn>200</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>Hz</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> for the strongest magnetic field we consider and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:mn>50</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>Hz</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> for the median case and is therefore detectable in principle by future GW observatories, which should include the magnetic field in their analyses. We also explore the impact of the magnetic field on the postmerger electromagnetic emission, and demonstrate that an extremely large magnetic field, or alternatively a significant shear viscosity mechanism, can cause a supramassive NS remnant to collapse to a black hole in less than 100 ms and lead to jet formation, although we do not expect the conditions for such an outcome to be realistic.

</details>

Abstract: Pulsar timing array experiments have reported evidence for a stochastic background of nanohertz gravitational waves consistent with the signal expected from a population of supermassive black hole binaries. Their analyses assume power-law spectra for intrinsic pulsar noise and for the background, as well as a Hellings-Downs cross-correlation pattern among the gravitational-wave-induced residuals across pulsars. These assumptions may not be realized in actuality. We test them in the NANOGrav 15 yr dataset using Bayesian posterior predictive checks. After fitting our fiducial model to real data, we generate a population of simulated dataset replications. We use the replications to assess whether the optimal statistic significance, interpulsar correlations, and spectral coefficients are extreme. We recover Hellings-Downs correlations in simulated datasets at significance levels consistent with the correlations measured in the NANOGrav 15 yr dataset. A similar test on spectral coefficients shows that their values in real data are not extreme compared to their distributions across replications. We also evaluate the evidence for the stochastic background using posterior predictive versions of the frequentist optimal statistic and of Bayesian model comparison and find comparable significance (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>3.2</mml:mn><mml:mi>σ</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>3</mml:mn><mml:mi>σ</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> respectively) to what was previously reported for the standard statistics. We conclude with novel visualizations of the reconstructed gravitational waveforms that enter the residuals for each pulsar. Our analysis strengthens confidence in the identification and characterization of the gravitational-wave background.

</details>


### [36] [Cross-comparison of sampling algorithms for pulse profile modeling of PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>0740</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>6620</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.13682)
*Hoogkamer, Mariska,Kini, Yves,Salmi, Tuomo,Watts, Anna L.,Buchner, Johannes*

TL;DR: 该文针对高质量脉冲星 PSR J0740+6620 的脉冲轮廓建模，比较 X-PSI 中 MultiNest 与 UltraNest 两种嵌套采样算法的表现。结果显示，在模拟数据上两种采样器均能给出准确且无偏的参数估计，在真实数据上也能产生一致的置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NICER 数据已使多颗脉冲星的质量和半径推断成为可能，并由此为致密核物质状态方程提供线索，这一过程依赖脉冲轮廓建模。由于这些结果重要，需要对推断流程进行仔细验证并检验其稳健性，因此本文考察采样器选择对 X-PSI 推断的影响。

Method: 摘要未说明具体观测或数据获取细节；所述研究使用模拟最近所分析的 PSR J0740+6620 的 NICER 和 XMM-Newton 数据集的合成数据，评估 X-PSI 中 MultiNest 的多模态嵌套采样算法与 UltraNest 的切片嵌套采样算法的参数恢复性能、收敛性和计算成本，并进一步针对 PSR J0740+6620 的真实数据研究推断后果。

Result: 摘要报告，在分析模拟数据时，MultiNest 的多模态嵌套采样算法和 UltraNest 的切片嵌套采样算法均能可靠运行，产生准确且无偏的参数估计结果；对于 PSR J0740+6620 的真实数据，两种采样器产生一致的置信区间。摘要未说明具体数值、不确定性或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 作者明确支持的结论是：对于 PSR J0740+6620 的脉冲轮廓建模，所比较的 MultiNest 与 UltraNest 采样算法均表现可靠，模拟数据上参数估计准确且无偏，真实数据上置信区间一致。就可迁移性而言，摘要未说明该方法是否可直接用于其他脉冲星或单脉冲、模式转换、计时、自转减慢、偏振、射电谱或 FRB 等相关研究；根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: In the past few years, NICER data has enabled mass and radius inferences for various pulsars, and thus shed light on the equation of state for dense nuclear matter. This is achieved through a technique called pulse profile modeling. The importance of the results necessitates careful validation and testing of the robustness of the inference procedure. In this paper, we investigate the effect of sampler choice for X-PSI (x-ray pulse simulation and inference), an open-source package for pulse profile modeling and Bayesian statistical inference that has been used extensively for analysis of NICER data. We focus on the specific case of the high-mass pulsar PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>0740</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>6620</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>. Using synthetic data that mimics the most recently analyzed NICER and XMM-Newton datasets of PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>0740</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>6620</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, we evaluate the parameter recovery performance, convergence, and computational cost for MultiNest's multimodal nested sampling algorithm and UltraNest's slice nested sampling algorithm. We find that both samplers perform reliably, producing accurate and unbiased parameter estimation results when analyzing simulated data. We also investigate the consequences for inference using the real data for PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>0740</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>6620</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, finding that both samplers produce consistent credible intervals.

</details>

O.,Zhu, Z.-H.,Zimmerman, A. B.,Zimmermann, L.,Zucker, M. E.,Zweizig, J.,The Ligo Scientific Collaboration, The VIRGO Collaboration,,The Kagra Collaboration*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 在 Advanced LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA 第四次观测运行前八个月（O4a）数据中，对 16--4096 Hz 频段内持续至多几秒的短时长引力波暂现源的搜寻未发现除已识别的双黑洞并合候选体之外的统计显著证据；相较此前搜寻，灵敏度有 2 至 10 倍改进，并且对与脉冲星 glitch 相关的中子星 \(f\)-模，假设源类似 Vela 脉冲星，本可探测到分数频率变化小至约 2 至 \(6\times10^{-5}\) 的 glitch 信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对短时长引力波暂现源开展全天空搜寻，关注 Advanced LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA 第四次观测运行 O4a 数据。其科学背景是此类暂现源可能持续至多几秒，并位于 16--4096 Hz 频段。摘要未明确说明具体的知识空白。

Method: 使用 Advanced LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA 第四次观测运行前八个月（O4a）的数据，采用四种对 16--4096 Hz 频段内持续至多几秒信号敏感的分析，排除低延迟分析已识别的双黑洞并合候选体。通过正弦-高斯、高斯脉冲和白噪声暴等代表性信号测量灵敏度，采用每 100 年 1 次虚警率作为探测阈值。还评估核心坍缩超新星模型，并考虑与脉冲星 glitch 相关的中子星 \(f\)-模。

Result: 在排除已识别的双黑洞并合候选体后，未发现其他引力波暂现源的统计显著证据。对正弦-高斯、高斯脉冲和白噪声暴等代表性信号的灵敏度测量显示，相较于此前搜寻，应变振幅灵敏度及源数密度 90% 置信上限有 2 至 10 倍改进。对于某些核心坍缩超新星模型，该搜寻本可以探测到整个银河系内恒星核心坍缩产生的引力波。对于与脉冲星 glitch 相关的中子星 \(f\)-模，假设源类似 Vela 脉冲星，该搜寻本可以探测到分数频率变化小至约 2 至 \(6\times10^{-5}\) 的 glitch 引力波信号，取决于中子星质量。

Conclusion: 作者报告在 O4a 数据中未发现除已识别双黑洞并合候选体外的统计显著引力波暂现源，并给出相较此前搜寻在应变振幅灵敏度和源数密度 90% 置信上限方面 2 至 10 倍的改进。对于某些核心坍缩超新星模型，该搜寻本可以探测到整个银河系内的恒星核心坍缩引力波；对于与脉冲星 glitch 相关的中子星 \(f\)-模，假设源类似 Vela 脉冲星，本可探测到分数频率变化小至约 2 至 \(6\times10^{-5}\) 的信号，取决于中子星质量。上述结果涉及脉冲星 glitch 与中子星 \(f\)-模，但并未涉及单脉冲、模式转换、偏振、射电谱、快速射电暴或磁星；其对磁星与快速射电暴研究的可迁移性，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: We present a search for short-duration gravitational-wave transients in data from the first eight months of Advanced LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA's fourth observing run, denoted O4a. We use four analyses which are sensitive to a wide range of potential signals lasting up to a few seconds in the 16--4096 Hz band. Excluding binary black hole merger candidates that were already identified by low-latency analyses, we find no statistically significant evidence for other gravitational-wave transients. We measure the sensitivity of the search for representative signals, including sine-Gaussians, Gaussian pulses, and white-noise bursts with different frequencies and durations, adopting a false alarm rate of 1 per 100 years as detection threshold. Depending on signal type, we find improvements over previous searches by factors of 2 to 10 in terms of sensitivity to strain amplitude and of 90% confidence upper limit on the rate density of sources. We also evaluate a variety of core-collapse supernova models and find that, for some models, the search could have detected gravitational waves from stellar core-collapse throughout the Milky Way. Finally, we consider neutron star <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>f</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>-modes associated with pulsar glitches and find that, assuming a source similar to the Vela Pulsar, the search could have detected a gravitational-wave signal from a glitch with fractional frequency change as small as <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula> to <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>6</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>5</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> depending on the neutron star mass.

</details>


### [38] [Long-time 3D supernova simulations of nonrotating progenitors with magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01155)
*Sykes, Bailey,Müller, Bernhard*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 在5个非旋转前身星的三维磁流体力学核心坍缩超新星模拟中，4个模型爆炸、1个失败；磁场未显著改变爆炸能增长，中子星反冲速度不超过300 km s^{-1}且外推自转周期为45 ms至1.8 s，磁力矩仅贡献约10%的自转加速，且未产生自转-反冲对齐机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望研究磁场对非旋转前身星核心坍缩超新星爆炸及遗迹性质可能产生的影响，并将模拟结果与先前非磁性模型及观测到的脉冲星速度、诞生自转速率进行比较。摘要指出，与先前模拟不同，增益区磁场放大水平较低，作者尝试解释其原因。

Method: 对质量介于9.5 M_⊙与24 M_⊙之间的非旋转前身星开展5个三维磁流体力学核心坍缩超新星模拟；4个爆炸模型被推进到反弹后0.9 s至1.6 s之间；分析诊断爆炸能、中子星反冲速度、外推中子星自转周期、磁力矩贡献、表面偶极磁场、均方根场强以及增益区磁场与动能均分的关系。摘要未说明具体的磁场初始化方式、数值分辨率或代码名称。

Result: 5个模型中有4个产生爆炸，1个失败。爆炸模型的诊断爆炸能以与先前非磁性模型相似的速度增长，达到0.11 foe至0.61 foe，模拟结束时仍在增长。中子星反冲速度不超过300 km s^{-1}，也仍在增长，但不太可能与观测到的脉冲星速度相冲突。外推中子星自转周期为45 ms至1.8 s，与观测到的诞生自转速率一致；磁力矩仅贡献约10%的自转加速。模拟中引入磁场未提供自转-反冲对齐机制。表面偶极磁场为10^{12}--10^{13} G，远小于均方根场强。增益区磁场至多达到动能均分的O(1%)，作者认为这可能是因为相对较早的激波复苏切断了吸积作为场放大功率来源的作用，而场放大似乎主要由增益区底部的剪切流驱动。

Conclusion: 作者认为，在这些非旋转前身星的三维磁流体力学模拟中，磁场并未显著改变爆炸能增长，所得中子星反冲速度和自转周期与观测到的脉冲星速度及诞生自转速率不冲突，且未提供自转-反冲对齐机制。表面偶极磁场远小于均方根场强；增益区磁场较弱，可能由于相对较早的激波复苏切断了吸积供能，而场放大似乎主要由增益区底部剪切流驱动。这些结果与脉冲星观测的关联仅体现在反冲速度和诞生自转速率比较上；对磁星或快速射电暴的物理联系，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: We perform five 3D magnetohydrodynamic core-collapse supernova simulations for nonrotating progenitors between <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>9.5</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>24</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>M</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>. Four of the five models produce explosions while one fails. The exploding models are extended to between 0.9 s and 1.6 s postbounce to study a possible impact of magnetic fields on explosion and remnant properties. Diagnostic explosion energies grow at a similar pace as in previous nonmagnetic models. They reach between 0.11 foe and 0.61 foe, but are still growing by the end of the simulations. Neutron star kicks reach no more than <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>300</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>km</mml:mi><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>s</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, and although these are also still growing, they are unlikely to be in conflict with observed pulsar velocities. Extrapolated neutron star spin periods are between 45 ms and 1.8 s, consistent with observed birth spin rates. Magnetic torques only contribute about 10% to the spin-up of the neutron star. The inclusion of magnetic fields does not provide a mechanism for spin-kick alignment in our simulations. Abstract: In the gravitational-wave analysis of pulsar-timing-array datasets, parameter estimation is usually performed using Markov chain Monte Carlo methods to explore posterior probability densities. Abstract: The recent announcement of evidence for a stochastic background of gravitational waves (GWB) in pulsar timing array (PTA) data has piqued interest across the scientific community. However, the complexity of individual pulsar noise models and the variety of modeling tools used for different types of pulsars present significant challenges for a truly unified analysis. In this work we propose a novel approach to the analysis of PTA data: first a posterior distribution over Fourier modes is produced for each pulsar individually. Method: 使用 Commensal Real-time ASKAP Fast Transient（CRAFT）巡天的 FRB 宿主星系样本，其中多数宿主已用甚大望远镜（VLT）上的 FORS2 在光学波段成像。用形态学软件 GALFIT 拟合光学和近红外成像所导出的参数化形态，得到 CRAFT 样本中 34 个由 ASKAP 探测并随后用 VLT 成像跟进的宿主的椭圆轴比 q（作为倾角的代理），并针对宿主星系倾角开展统计检验；估计 DMHost 还要求已知每个宿主的红移。

Result: 对 CRAFT 样本 34 个 FRB 宿主星系的分析未发现对 edge-on 星系中 FRB 探测的偏差；相反，发现了一个 FRB 宿主星系倾角与 DMHost 之间的新关系。利用该关系，宿主样本围绕 Macquart 关系的离散度被降低 40%。

Conclusion: 作者认为，所揭示的宿主星系倾角与 DMHost 之间的关系与 CRAFT FRB 主要起源于星系盘的解释一致，并据此提出一种仅需要深度光学成像来估计 DMHost 的新经验方法。根据摘要无法判断该关系是否可推广至 CRAFT 样本之外的 FRB 群体。

Abstract: It is commonly thought that fast radio bursts (FRBs) trace star formation, and that they are produced by magnetars. Both hypotheses imply that their sources are hosted primarily (but not exclusively) in galactic disks. Although there is evidence in that direction, it has yet to be shown conclusively. In addition, a tension exists with the fact that at least one FRB source is found in a globular cluster far from the disk of the host. If FRBs originate primarily in disks, the galaxy orientation could leave an impression in the sample of observed FRBs, either as a selection bias against FRBs in edge-on galaxies or as a correlation with DMHost. Statistical tests against the inclinations of FRB host galaxies, derived from parametric morphology applied to optical and near-infrared imaging, can search for these effects. Such a search requires an adequately large sample of known FRB hosts that have been imaged to a sufficient depth, while estimates of DMHost also require the redshift of each host to be known. These conditions are best met in the Commensal Real-time ASKAP Fast Transient (CRAFT) survey sample of FRB host galaxies, for which the majority have been imaged at optical wavelengths with FORS2 on the Very Large Telescope (VLT). Here these data are fitted with the morphology software GALFIT to derive the elliptical axis ratio q (a proxy for inclination) for 34 hosts from the CRAFT sample, detected with ASKAP and followed up with VLT imaging. We find no bias against FRB detections from edge-on galaxies; instead, we uncover a new relationship between the inclination of an FRB host galaxy and DMHost. This is consistent with a primarily disk-based origin for CRAFT FRBs. Using this relationship we are able to reduce the scatter of the host sample about the Macquart relation by 40%, demonstrating a new empirical method for estimating DMHost that requires only deep optical imaging.

</details>

Method: 摘要说明该工作利用脉冲星计时延迟的完整协方差矩阵评估当前与未来脉冲星计时阵列对各向异性的灵敏度，并给出公开可用的 Python 代码 fastPTA 用于预测 PTA 配置的约束能力。具体观测量、仪器、频段、时间跨度、样本与统计方法：摘要未说明。
