Abstract: We study the growth of supermassive black holes accounting for both accretion and mergers. The former is informed by observations of the quasar luminosity function (QLF) and the latter by the gravitational wave-background (GWB) recently detected by pulsar timing arrays, while estimates of the present-day black hole mass function provide a boundary condition. The GWB is dominated by the most massive black holes (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>≳</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mn>9</mml:mn></mml:msup><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>). We show that their evolution can be simplified into a two-step process: mergers dominate at <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>z</mml:mi><mml:mo>&lt;=</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>, while accretion peaks at <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>1.4</mml:mn><mml:mo>&lt;=</mml:mo><mml:mi>z</mml:mi><mml:mo>&lt;=</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>. The large amplitude of the observed GWB suggests a significant number of mergers. We show that this generically implies a higher average Eddington ratio for quasars relative to a scenario in which mergers are negligible. In the absence of mergers, matching local estimates of black hole abundance to the QLF implies a radiative efficiency of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>∊</mml:mi><mml:mi>r</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.12</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula> and an Eddington ratio of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>λ</mml:mi><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.2</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>. With mergers, a progenitor of mass <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mi>i</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> is boosted to a final total mass <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mi>f</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>, and there is a direct relation between the mass gained in mergers and the average Eddington ratio of the quasar population, given by <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mi>f</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>/</mml:mo><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mi>i</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mover><mml:mi>q</mml:mi><mml:mo>̄</mml:mo></mml:mover><mml:msup><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>N</mml:mi></mml:msup><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:mi>λ</mml:mi><mml:mo>/</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.2</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>, where <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mover><mml:mi>q</mml:mi><mml:mo>̄</mml:mo></mml:mover></mml:math></inline-formula> is the average mass ratio, and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>N</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> is the average number of mergers. There is thus a tension between the observed GWB, quasar properties, and the BH mass function: estimates of the mass function consistent with Eddington ratios inferred in quasars and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>∊</mml:mi><mml:mi>r</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.1</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula> underpredict the GWB; multiple/equal mass mergers can boost the GWB but lead to a high Eddington ratio. If the local mass function is on the high end of current estimates, the GWB is more readily explained but requires low efficiencies <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>∊</mml:mi><mml:mi>r</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> not expected in standard luminous accretion models. The significant merger rate implied by the GWB also strongly suggests that the most massive BHs in the local universe have significant spin due to the orbital angular momentum from mergers, perhaps <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>a</mml:mi><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.5</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>.

</details>


### [94] [Distribution of the gravitational-wave background from supermassive black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.17010)
*Sato-Polito, Gabriela,Zaldarriaga, Matias*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该工作重新分析NANOGrav的15年数据集，利用完整频谱信息指出当前引力波测量需要比本地观测建议多约10倍的黑洞，并倾向于对背景有贡献的典型质量≲10^10 M_⊙的质量函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTA合作组最近探测到引力波，但其信号起源与源性质尚不明确。由旋近超大质量黑洞产生的背景振幅可根据今日并合遗迹预测，却显著低于PTA测量值，需要极端条件才能勉强相符。作者希望利用PTA数据的完整频谱信息，重新评估这一差异。

Method: 摘要说明：重新审视NANOGrav的15年数据集，并利用PTA数据捕获的完整频谱信息；讨论离散点源数目因泊松涨落导致的频谱偏差；推导给定SMBH模型下特征应变分布的简单表达式；使用基于本地质量函数的最小模型重新拟合NANOGrav自由频谱。其他观测或分析细节摘要未说明。

Result: 作者重新拟合NANOGrav自由频谱后表明，当前引力波测量需要比本地观测所建议的多约10倍的黑洞；结果不利于由少数极重源主导的质量函数；对背景有贡献的典型质量≲10^10 M_⊙。作者还基于与各向同性背景相符的一系列SMBH模型，探讨在当前灵敏度下通常有多少源可被单独探测。

Conclusion: 作者认为，观测频谱相对平均h_c^2∝f^{-4/3}的偏差可能源于贡献背景的离散点源引起的泊松涨落，因而除背景振幅外还能提供源population的信息；基于本地质量函数的最小模型拟合显示当前测量需要的黑洞数量多于本地观测，且不倾向于由少数极重源主导。与脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、模式转换、FRB等具体联系，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: The recent detection of gravitational waves (GWs) by pulsar timing array (PTA) collaborations spurred a variety of questions regarding the origin of the signal and the properties of its sources. The amplitude of a GW background produced by inspiralling supermassive black holes (SMBHs) can be predicted in a relatively robust manner from the present-day merged remnants, observed as single SMBHs at the centers of galaxies, but falls short of the signal measured by PTAs by a significant amount, requiring equal mass mergers, extremely short delay times, and no accretion in order to achieve a modest consistency. In this work, we revisit NANOGrav's 15-yr dataset and reassess the aforementioned discrepancy using the full spectral information captured by PTA data. As previously noted in the literature, the discrete number of point sources contributing to the background may lead to deviations in the observed spectrum relative to the average (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msubsup><mml:mi>h</mml:mi><mml:mi>c</mml:mi><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:msubsup><mml:mo>∝</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mi>f</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>4</mml:mn><mml:mo>/</mml:mo><mml:mn>3</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula>) due to Poisson fluctuations, providing additional information about the source population beyond the background amplitude. We derive a simple expression for the characteristic strain distribution given a SMBH model, which is generally applicable regardless of the method used to model the black hole population. We then refit the NANOGrav free spectrum using a minimal model based on the local mass function, showing that the current GW measurement requires roughly <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula> times more black holes than suggested by local observations and disfavors mass functions dominated by few very heavy sources, with the typical mass that contributes to the background <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:msup><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>. Given the range of SMBH models found to be consistent with the isotropic background, we address what is the typical number sources that would be individually detectable, given the current sensitivity.

</details>


### [95] [Measuring Cosmic Expansion with Diffractive Gravitational Scintillation of Nanohertz Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.03214)
*Jow, Dylan L.,Pen, Ue-Li*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该文提出，每个脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）源都会被视线横向方向上可能数百个星系盘衍射透镜，导致应变出现约10^{-3} λ_{1 pc}^{-1}量级的适度调制；相干PTA可分辨由此产生的闪烁图样，结合前景红移信息后或可用于精确测量宇宙膨胀，单个PTA源有望推断出数百个红移-距离对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超长波长引力波通过脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）被发现的进展，为基础科学开辟了新途径。在此背景下，本文关注的问题是：PTA源的引力波信号在传播途中是否会受到前景星系盘的衍射透镜影响，以及这种效应能否被利用于宇宙膨胀测量。摘要未说明既有研究在利用此类透镜效应测量宇宙膨胀方面的具体空白。

Method: 摘要为理论/分析性陈述，未详细说明所用数据或观测过程。文中指出每个PTA源会被视线横向方向上可能数百个星系盘衍射透镜，并讨论波动透镜效应导致的应变调制；所诱导的干涉或闪烁图样被认为可由相干PTA分辨，并可与前景红移信息结合使用。摘要未说明具体望远镜、频段、时间跨度、样本、数值模拟或统计分析方法。

Result: 摘要报告的核心结果是：每个PTA源都会被视线横向方向上可能数百个星系盘衍射透镜，波动透镜效应导致应变出现适度调制，量级为∆h/h ~ 10^{-3} λ_{1 pc}^{-1}。该调制所诱导的干涉（闪烁）图样将可被相干PTA分辨。摘要还指出，若未来PTA实验能达到足够信噪比以探测这些小的调制，则可能从单个PTA源的衍射透镜中推断出数百个红移-距离对。摘要未给出统计显著性或具体探测事件。

Conclusion: 作者认为，由星系盘衍射透镜引起的应变调制及其干涉（闪烁）图样，可与前景红移信息一起被用于对宇宙膨胀进行精确测量；若未来PTA实验达到足够信噪比，单个PTA源即可能提供数百个红移-距离对。这一结论是基于波动透镜效应的预期性推断。对于该效应能否直接联系到脉冲星单脉冲发射、磁星、快速射电暴（FRB）、脉冲轮廓演化、模式转换或偏振等具体观测现象，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: The recent discovery of ultralong wavelength gravitational waves through the advent of pulsar timing arrays (PTA) has opened up new avenues for fundamental science. Here we show that every PTA source will be diffractively lensed by potentially hundreds of galactic disks transverse to its line of sight, leading to modest modulations in the strain, <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>∆</mml:mi><mml:mi>h</mml:mi><mml:mo>/</mml:mo><mml:mi>h</mml:mi><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>3</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>λ</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>pc</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, due to wave lensing effects. The induced interference, or scintillation, pattern will be resolvable by coherent PTAs and may be leveraged, alongside foreground redshift information, to make precise measurements of cosmic expansion. If future PTA experiments can achieve enough signal to noise to detect these small modulations, hundreds of redshift-distance pairs may be inferred from the diffractive lensing of an individual PTA source.

</details>


### [96] [Fast Bayesian method for coherent gravitational wave searches with relative astrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19206)
*Zhang, Benjamin,Pardo, Kris,Wang, Yijun,Bouma, Luke,Chang, Tzu-Ching,Doré, Olivier*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该文将脉冲星计时阵中通过预计算贝叶斯似然内积来加速搜索的技术推广至相对恒星天体测量数据集，并据此给出 Kepler 与 Roman 对相干引力波灵敏度的预报，指出由于天体测量基线的角天区覆盖较低，相干引力波源在天区上定位很差。在 $10^{-8}$ Hz 至 $10^{-6}$ Hz，Roman 对瞬时应变高于 $h_0\simeq10^{-11.4}$、Kepler 对高于 $h_0\simeq10^{-12.4}$ 的相干引力波敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微赫兹频段目前没有专用探测器，利用相对恒星天体测量探测相干引力波是该频段一种有前景的方法。与其他引力波探测技术相比，天体测量处于极高基线数与每基线低信噪比的极限，使用常规贝叶斯搜索技术会带来计算困难；因此需要一种能高效搜索此类数据的方法。

Method: 作者通过预计算贝叶斯似然中的内积，扩展了一种用于高效搜索脉冲星计时阵数据集的技术，并表明该技术适用于天体测量数据集。该技术可将总数据集大小最多减少 $O(100)$ 倍，同时在 2 个数量级的引力波频率范围内保持优于 1% 的精度。作者将该技术应用于 Kepler Space Telescope 和 Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope 任务的模拟天体测量数据集，获得这些任务对相干引力波的灵敏度预报。

Result: 由于天体测量基线的角天区覆盖较低，相干引力波源在天区上定位很差。从 $10^{-8}$ Hz 到 $10^{-6}$ Hz，Roman 对瞬时应变高于 $h_0\simeq10^{-11.4}$ 的相干引力波敏感，Kepler 对高于 $h_0\simeq10^{-12.4}$ 的应变敏感。在该应变下，对于频率为 $10^{-7}$ Hz、啁啾质量为 $10^9M_\odot$ 的源，Kepler 可在光度距离 3.6 Mpc 处探测到，Roman 可在 0.3 Mpc 处探测到。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：其加速贝叶斯搜索技术可应用于天体测量数据集，并据此给出 Kepler 与 Roman 对相干引力波的灵敏度预报；由于天体测量基线的角天区覆盖较低，相干引力波源在天区上定位很差。作者最后讨论了改进这些应变阈值的可能策略。根据摘要无法判断该工作与脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、快速射电暴或射电偏振等具体课题的物理联系。

Abstract: Using relative stellar astrometry for the detection of coherent gravitational wave sources is a promising method for the microhertz range, where no dedicated detectors currently exist. Compared to other gravitational wave detection techniques, astrometry operates in an extreme high-baseline-number and low-signal-to-noise ratio per baseline limit, which leads to computational difficulties when using conventional Bayesian search techniques. We extend a technique for efficiently searching pulsar timing array datasets through the precomputation of inner products in the Bayesian likelihood, showing that it is applicable to astrometric datasets. Using this technique, we are able to reduce the total dataset size by up to a factor of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>O</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>100</mml:mn><mml:mo>)</mml:mo></mml:math></inline-formula>, while remaining accurate to within 1% over 2 orders of magnitude in gravitational wave frequency. Applying this technique to simulated astrometric datasets for the Kepler Space Telescope and Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope missions, we obtain forecasts for the sensitivity of these missions to coherent gravitational waves. Due to the low angular sky coverage of astrometric baselines, we find that coherent gravitational wave sources are poorly localized on the sky. Despite this, from <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>8</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>Hz</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> to <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>6</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>Hz</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>, we find that Roman is sensitive to coherent gravitational waves with an instantaneous strain above <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>h</mml:mi><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>≃</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>11.4</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula>, and Kepler is sensitive to strains above <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>h</mml:mi><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:msub><mml:mo>≃</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>12.4</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula>. At this strain, we can detect a source with a frequency of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>7</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>Hz</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> and a chirp mass of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mn>9</mml:mn></mml:msup><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> at a luminosity distance of 3.6 Mpc for Kepler and 0.3 Mpc for Roman. We finally discuss possible strategies for improving on these strain thresholds.

</details>


### [97] [Mitigating cosmic variance in the Hellings-Downs curve: A cosmic microwave background analogy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.12073)
*Pitrou, Cyril,Cusin, Giulia*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 本文用与CMB类似的多极谐波与二次估计量框架分析PTA的Hellings-Downs关联，表明既往所称的宇宙方差并非PTA测量的内禀限制，可通过增加观测时间或提高采样率加以减轻。预测约200颗脉冲星监测25年可测得HD关联中直至$\ell=4$的多极。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PTA通过Hellings-Downs关联探测随机引力波背景，而此前文献中把所谓的宇宙方差视为限制。本文借鉴CMB温度涨落关联的理论框架，旨在厘清该方差是否为PTA测量的内禀限制，并讨论最优估计下的可测多极范围及与CMB的类比。

Method: 摘要表明：采用紧循CMB温度涨落关联计算传统的谐波形式推导HD关联；给出模型拟合所致透射函数的解析估计；考察频率空间协方差矩阵的非对角性；在多极空间构建HD关联的二次估计量，分别针对理想实验和现实的脉冲星噪声模型，并在噪声加权后得到依赖于多极的有效频率数。摘要未说明具体仪器、频段、数据来源与统计或仿真细节。

Result: 摘要报告：频率空间的协方差矩阵变得非常非对角；对理想实验，信噪比随观测时间与频率bin数增长，而频率bin数由采样率决定；对非理想实验行为类似，有效频率数依赖于多极并经噪声加权得到。预测以约200颗脉冲星监测25年，可测量HD关联中直至$\ell=4$的多极。此外，若原初张量涨落功率谱很蓝（$n_T>4$），则这些张量模式导致的CMB角关联也将呈现HD关联。

Conclusion: 作者认为，此前文献所称的宇宙方差不是PTA测量的内禀限制；使用最优估计量时，可通过积累更多观测时间或改进脉冲星监测的采样率来减轻，并将在未来实验中继续减小，这与CMB角关联中宇宙方差为不可约约束不同。作者还指出，在$n_T>4$的很蓝原初张量谱情形下，这些张量模式导致的CMB角关联也会表现出HD关联。结果针对各向同性引力子浴推导，并讨论了引力子分布函数各向异性的情况。根据摘要无法判断该框架对其他观测或分析的具体适用性。

Abstract: The Hellings-Downs (HD) correlation, which characterizes the signature of a stochastic gravitational wave background measured via pulsar timing arrays (PTA), is derived using a harmonic formalism. This approach closely follows the theoretical framework traditionally employed to compute correlations of temperature fluctuations in the cosmic microwave background (CMB). This parallel enables a direct comparison between the correlations observed in PTA and those in CMB experiments. After providing analytic estimates of the transmission functions due to the model-fitting procedure, we show that the covariance matrix in frequency space becomes very nondiagonal. We then build formally the quadratic estimator for the HD correlation in multipolar space, for both a perfect experiment, and for a realistic pulsar noise model. For a perfect experiment, we show that the signal to noise ratio grows with the observation time and the number of frequency bins, in turn determined by the cadence of observation. For an imperfect experiment, the behavior is similar, with an effective multipole-dependent number of frequency, obtained after weighting with noise. We predict that with <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:mn>200</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> pulsars monitored for 25 years, multipoles of the HD correlation up to <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>ℓ</mml:mo><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>4</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula> can be measured. Our findings clarify that what has been referred to as cosmic variance in some of the previous literature is not an intrinsic limitation for PTA measurements. Instead, when an optimal estimator is used, this variance can be mitigated by accumulating more observation time or improving the cadence of pulsar monitoring. Therefore, unlike CMB angular correlations, where cosmic variance represents an irreducible constraint, it can be reduced in PTA measurements and will continue to diminish in future experiments. Finally, we show that if the primordial power spectrum of tensor fluctuations was very blue with <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mi>T</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>&gt;</mml:mo><mml:mn>4</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>, the CMB angular correlation due to these tensor modes would also exhibit a HD correlation. While our results are derived for the case of an isotropic bath of gravitons, we also discuss the case in which the graviton distribution function is anisotropic.

</details>

*Zwick, Lorenz,Soyuer, Deniz,D'Orazio, Daniel J.,O'Neill, David,Derdzinski, Andrea,Saha, Prasenjit,Blas, Diego,Jenkins, Alexander C.,Kelley, Luke Zoltan*

Method: 摘要说明：研究利用天王星轨道器与探测器任务的无线电多普勒跟踪设备，在其行星际巡航期间进行观测；发展了一种叠加跟踪数据并考虑随时间变化探测器几何构型的方法，用以构建任务在 3×10^{-9} Hz 至 10^{-4} Hz 频段对引力波的灵敏度。摘要还提到使用两个独立的族群模型进行预测，并评估对天体物理和原初随机引力波背景以及超轻 DM 模型的灵敏度。具体跟踪时段、样本量、噪声处理细节与统计分析方法摘要未说明。

Result: 摘要报告：该方法给出的灵敏度覆盖 3×10^{-9} Hz 至 10^{-4} Hz；该任务有潜力填补脉冲星计时与天基干涉测量引力波天文台之间的空白；若将 Cassini 时代噪声降低 O(10) 倍，使用两个独立族群模型预测可探测到 O(few) 个单个大质量黑洞双星；此外给出了对天体物理和原初随机引力波背景的灵敏度，以及检验甚至通过探测确认超轻 DM 模型的能力；跟踪航天器的行星际巡航可覆盖尚未探索的参数空间区域。具体数值、不确定度与统计显著性摘要未说明。

Conclusion: 作者明确支持的结论是：天王星轨道器与探测器任务的多普勒跟踪有潜力填补脉冲星计时与天基干涉测量引力波天文台之间的频段空白；在噪声改善 O(10) 倍的条件下可探测到 O(few) 个单个大质量黑洞双星，并可对随机引力波背景和超轻 DM 模型给出灵敏度或检验能力；相关跟踪将覆盖尚未探索的参数空间。至于该工作与脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲发射、脉冲轮廓演化、模式转换、偏振或 FRB 的具体联系，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: With the recent announcement by NASA's Planetary Science and Astrobiology Decadal Survey 2023-2032, a priority flagship mission to the planet Uranus is anticipated. Here, we explore the prospects of using the mission's radio Doppler tracking equipment to detect gravitational waves (GWs) and other analogous signals related to dark matter (DM) over the duration of its interplanetary cruise. We develop a methodology to stack tracking data and account for time varying detector geometry, thereby constructing the sensitivity of the mission to GWs over the wide frequency range of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>3</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>9</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>Hz</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> to <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>4</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>Hz</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>. We find that the mission has the potential to fill the gap between pulsar timing and space-based-interferometry GW observatories. If improvements in reducing Cassini era noise by a factor of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>O</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mo>)</mml:mo></mml:math></inline-formula> are implemented, we forecast the detection of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>O</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mi>few</mml:mi><mml:mo>)</mml:mo></mml:math></inline-formula> individual massive black hole binaries using two independent population models. Additionally, we determine the mission's sensitivity to both astrophysical and primordial stochastic gravitational wave backgrounds, as well as its capacity to test, or even confirm via detection, ultralight DM models. In all these cases, the tracking of the spacecraft over its interplanetary cruise would enable coverage of unexplored regions of parameter space, where signals from new phenomena in our Universe may be lurking.

</details>


### [99] [Search for continuous gravitational wave signals from luminous dark photon superradiance clouds with LVK O3 observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.02052)
*Mirasola, Lorenzo,Mondino, Cristina,Amicucci, Francesco,Siemonsen, Nils,Palomba, Cristiano,D'Antonio, Sabrina,Leaci, Paola,D'Onofrio, Luca,Astone, Pia,Egaña-Ugrinovic, Daniel,Huang, Junwu,Baryakhtar, Masha,East, William E.*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该研究对34个测量良好且此前未被作为目标的源，利用LVK第三次观测运行数据首次搜索来自银河系黑洞周围暗光子超辐射云的连续引力波（CW），未发现CW信号并给出95%置信水平应变振幅上限；结合先前限制解释后，这一无信号结果不利于约$10^{-13}$至$10^{-12}\ \mathrm{eV}/c^{2}$之间的一组离散暗光子质量及$10^{-9}$--$10^{-7}$的动理学混合耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 围绕旋转黑洞的动理学混合暗光子超辐射云可产生多信使电磁和引力波信号；对于恒星级质量黑洞，此类系统类似毫秒脉冲星，预计在LVK灵敏度波段发射脉动射电波和连续引力波。研究据此从现有脉冲星目录中选取潜在候选体，检验其CW发射并约束暗光子理论。摘要未说明更具体的知识空白。

Method: 从现有脉冲星目录中选取44个频率近似一致或频率漂移为正的源，作为古老银河系黑洞周围长寿命超辐射云的潜在候选体；其中34个测量良好且此前未被作为目标的源构成子集，利用LVK第三次观测运行数据首次开展CW发射搜索。摘要未说明具体望远镜、频段、时间跨度或统计分析方法。

Result: 对34个测量良好且此前未被作为目标的源，首次利用LVK第三次观测运行数据搜索CW发射，未发现CW信号证据，并对发射应变振幅给出95%置信水平上限。结合先前搜索的限制，通过分析银河系黑洞超辐射云的预期信号进行解释后发现，即使对于中等自旋黑洞，未观测到CW信号也不利于约$10^{-13}$至$10^{-12}\ \mathrm{eV}/c^{2}$之间的一组离散暗光子质量和$10^{-9}$--$10^{-7}$范围的动理学混合耦合，但取决于关于黑洞族群性质和云电磁辐射的假设。

Conclusion: 作者认为，未观测到CW信号的结果，结合先前搜索的限制，在所述假设下不利于特定离散暗光子质量和动理学混合耦合范围，这一解释取决于关于黑洞族群性质和云电磁辐射的假设。该结果对脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、脉冲轮廓演化、模式转换、脉冲星计时、自旋下降力矩变化、偏振、射电频谱、瞬变射电发射或磁星与快速射电暴联系的可迁移性，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Superradiance clouds of kinetically mixed dark photons around spinning black holes can produce observable multimessenger electromagnetic and gravitational wave signals. The cloud generates electric fields of up to a teravolt-per-meter, which leads to a cascade production of charged particles, yielding a turbulent quasiequilibrium plasma around the black hole, and resulting in electromagnetic fluxes ranging from supernova to pulsar-like luminosities. For stellar mass black holes, such systems resemble millisecond pulsars and are expected to emit pulsating radio waves and continuous gravitational waves (CWs) within the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) sensitivity band. We select 44 sources with approximately coincident frequencies or positive frequency drifts from existing pulsar catalogs as potential candidates of long-lasting superradiance clouds around old Galactic black holes. For a subset of 34 sources that are well measured and have not been previously targeted, we perform the first search for CW emission in LVK data from the third observing run. We find no evidence of a CW signal and place 95% confidence level upper limits on the emitted strain amplitude. We interpret these results, together with limits from previous searches, in terms of the underlying dark photon theory by performing an analysis of the expected signals from superradiance clouds from Galactic black holes. We find that, even for moderately spinning black holes, the absence of an observed CW signal disfavors a discrete set of dark photon masses between about <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>13</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>12</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>eV</mml:mi><mml:mo>/</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mi>c</mml:mi><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> and kinetic mixing couplings in the range of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>9</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mi>--</mml:mi><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>7</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, subject to assumptions about the properties of the black hole population and the cloud's electromagnetic emission.

</details>


### [100] [Primordial gravitational wave background as a probe of primordial black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10033)
*Kumar, Utkarsh*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该工作表明，用原初引力波解释脉冲星计时阵列探测到的随机引力波背景，对应质量范围 $[10^{-12}-10^{-3}]\,M_{\odot}$ 内的原初黑洞形成，且不会导致原初黑洞过量产生；由此受现有原初黑洞丰度限制约束的 PGW 振幅所预测的随机引力波背景信号，可被未来引力波天文台探测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时阵列已探测到随机引力波背景，其原初引力波解释是否与原初黑洞形成相容、是否会导致原初黑洞过量产生，是需要检验的问题。摘要指出该解释对应 $[10^{-12}-10^{-3}]\,M_{\odot}$ 质量范围的原初黑洞形成，因此需要结合现有原初黑洞丰度限制来考察其可行性。

Method: 摘要说明本文研究原初引力波诱导的密度扰动坍缩形成原初黑洞，并以脉冲星计时阵列探测到的随机引力波背景的原初引力波解释为出发点；进一步利用现有对宽质量范围内原初黑洞丰度的限制来推导原初引力波的振幅。具体的观测数据、仪器、频段、时间跨度、样本与统计方法，摘要未说明。

Result: 摘要指出，原初引力波解释对应质量范围 $[10^{-12}-10^{-3}]\,M_{\odot}$ 内的原初黑洞形成；分析显示该解释仍然可行，因为它不会导致原初黑洞过量产生。作者利用现有对宽质量范围原初黑洞丰度的限制推导出原初引力波的振幅，并指出这些受限制的振幅预测的随机引力波背景信号将可被未来的引力波天文台探测到。

Conclusion: 作者认为，原初引力波对近期脉冲星计时阵列数据的解释仍然可行，不会导致原初黑洞过量产生；由现有原初黑洞丰度限制得到的原初引力波振幅，所预测的随机引力波背景信号可为未来引力波天文台探测，从而为检验原初黑洞与原初引力波提供可能途径。关于该结论对脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、模式转换、偏振、射电谱或快速射电暴等具体课题的可迁移性，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: We study the formation of primordial black holes (PBHs) from the collapse of density perturbations induced by primordial gravitational waves (PGWs). The PGWs' interpretation of the stochastic gravitational wave background detected by the pulsar timing array corresponds to PBH formation in the mass range <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>[</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>12</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>3</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mo>]</mml:mo><mml:msub><mml:mi>M</mml:mi><mml:mo>⊙</mml:mo></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>. Importantly, our analysis shows that PGWs' interpretation of recent pulsar timing array data remains viable, as it does not lead to PBH overproduction. We derive the amplitude of PGWs by leveraging existing constraints on the PBH abundance across a wide mass range. Notably, these constrained amplitudes predict stochastic gravitational wave background signals that would be detectable by future gravitational wave observatories.

</details>


### [101] [Detecting gravitational wave anisotropies from supermassive black hole binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.08705)
*Lemke, Anna-Malin,Mitridate, Andrea,Gersbach, Kyle A.*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该工作表明，目前缺乏 GWB 各向异性探测与 SMBHB 起源并不矛盾：具有 NANOGrav 15 年数据集噪声特征的 PTA 仅有 2%--11% 的概率探测到 SMBHB 产生背景的各向异性偏离，而 IPTA DR3 预计将增至 4%--28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个 PTA 合作组已发现 GWB 证据，但其起源未知；SMBHB 是主要候选，宇宙学起源也被提出。SMBHB 产生背景的显著特征是由双星在本地宇宙分布导致的各向异性，但迄今各向异性搜寻均无探测。因此需要判断缺乏各向异性探测是否与 SMBHB 起源相矛盾。

Method: 摘要未说明具体的观测或数据分析方法；作者通过计算当前和未来 PTA 观测到偏离各向同性 GWB 的概率开展研究，并考虑具有 NANOGrav 15 年数据集噪声特征的 PTA 与 IPTA DR3 数据集，且概率依赖 SMBHB 种群性质。摘要未说明具体频率、时间跨度、样本量或统计方法。

Result: 具有 NANOGrav 15 年数据集噪声特征的 PTA 在 SMBHB 产生的 GWB 中探测到偏离各向同性的概率仅为 2%--11%，取决于 SMBHB 种群性质；对于 IPTA DR3 数据集，这些概率预计增至 4%--28%，在零探测情况下会对 SMBHB 解释施加更大压力。作者还识别出更可能产生显著各向异性偏离的 SMBHB 种群。

Conclusion: 作者认为，目前缺乏各向异性探测与 GWB 的 SMBHB 起源并不矛盾；若未来 IPTA DR3 仍无探测，概率上升将对 SMBHB 解释构成更大压力。识别出的更易产生各向异性偏离的 SMBHB 种群信息，可与 GWB 谱性质结合用于刻画 SMBHB 种群。根据摘要无法判断其与脉冲星单脉冲、模式转换、磁星或 FRB 的具体联系。

Abstract: Several pulsar timing array (PTA) collaborations have recently found evidence for a gravitational wave background (GWB) permeating our Galaxy. The origin of this background is still unknown. Indeed, while the gravitational wave emission from inspiraling supermassive black hole binaries (SMBHB) is the primary candidate for its origin, several cosmological sources have also been proposed. One distinctive feature of SMBHB-generated backgrounds is the presence of GWB anisotropies stemming from the binaries distribution in the local Universe. However, none of the anisotropy searches performed to date reported a detection. In this work, we show that the lack of anisotropy detection is not currently in tension with a SMBHB origin of the background. We accomplish this by calculating the probability for present and future PTAs to observe deviations from an isotropic GWB. We find that a PTA with the noise characteristics of the NANOGrav 15-year data set had only a 2%--11% probability of detecting deviations from isotropy in an SMBHB-generated GWB, depending on the properties of the SMBHB population. However, we estimate that for the IPTA DR3 data set these probabilities will increase to 4%--28%, putting more pressure on the SMBHB interpretation in case of a null detection. We also identify SMBHB populations that are more likely to produce significant deviations from isotropy. This information could be used together with the spectral properties of the GWB to characterize the SMBHB population.

</details>


### [102] [From eccentric binaries to nonstationary gravitational wave backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.01899)
*Falxa, Mikel,Leclere, Hippolyte Quelquejay,Sesana, Alberto*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该工作估计了由超大质量黑洞双星（SMBHB）族群产生的引力波背景（GWB）功率谱中时间依赖涨落的振幅随双星偏心率的依赖关系：均匀分布的等质量族群产生的非平稳性无法被当前 PTA 数据集探测，但更符合天体物理实际的族群，尤其是质量更大、偏心率更高的族群，可能产生很大且可探测的非平稳性，单个低 SNR（SNR≈1）偏心双星亦可导致显著时间涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏心双星辐射的 GW 功率强烈依赖于瞬时轨道相位，因此其功率谱随时间变化，由此产生的 GWB 可能呈现非平稳性。摘要指出，GWB 的性质直接取决于其组成成分的属性，而目前对于这种非平稳性随双星偏心率如何变化、以及在 PTA 观测中是否可探测，尚需定量估计。

Method: 摘要未说明具体的观测活动、数据处理流程或统计方法。根据摘要，作者通过估计 GWB 功率谱中时间依赖涨落的振幅，并将其表示为双星偏心率的函数，聚焦于超大质量黑洞双星（SMBHB）族群产生的、应可由脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）观测到的 GWB；并分别考虑均匀分布的等质量 SMBHB 族群与更符合天体物理动机的 SMBHB 族群。

Result: 作者表明，大量均匀分布的等质量 SMBHB 族群产生的非平稳特征无法被当前 PTA 数据集探测到。但使用更符合实际且由天体物理动机驱动的 SMBHB 族群时，非平稳性可能变得非常大且可探测，尤其是在质量更大、偏心率更高的族群情况下。摘要还指出，当其中一个双星比 GWB 稍亮时，时间涨落可以变得显著；对于相对于 GWB 信噪比低的单个双星系统（SNR≈1），情况同样如此，而标准数据分析方法将难以探测这类系统。摘要未给出具体的涨落幅度数值或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 作者认为，对 GWB 中非平稳特征的探测可能表明存在一些处于偏心轨道的相对明亮的 GW 源，从而为信号的起源提供新的见解；这一点对使用下一代射电望远镜观测的 PTA 尤为相关，因为这类观测将提供对 GWB 性质的高精度测量，并提高对时间依赖涨落的灵敏度。摘要未就磁星、快速射电暴（FRB）或射电单脉冲观测给出任何物理结论，根据摘要无法判断其与该领域的联系。

Abstract: A large population of binary systems in the Universe emitting gravitational waves (GW) would produce a stochastic noise, known as the gravitational wave background (GWB). The properties of the GWB directly depend on the attributes of its constituents. If the binary systems are in eccentric orbits, it is well established that the GW power they radiate strongly depends on their instantaneous orbital phase. Consequently, their power spectrum varies over time, and the resulting GWB can appear nonstationary. In this work, we estimate the amplitude of time-dependent fluctuations in the GWB power spectrum as a function of the eccentricity of the binaries. Specifically, we focus on the GWB produced by a population of supermassive black hole binaries (SMBHB) that should be observable by pulsar timing arrays (PTA). We show that a large population of homogeneously distributed equal SMBHBs produces nonstationary features that are undetectable by current PTA datasets. However, using more realistic and astrophysically motivated populations of SMBHBs, we show that the nonstationarity might become very large and detectable, especially in the case of more massive and eccentric populations. In particular, when one binary is slightly brighter than the GWB, we demonstrate that time fluctuations can become significant. This is also true for individual binary systems with a low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) relative to the GWB (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>SNR</mml:mi><mml:mo>≈</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>), which standard data analysis methods would struggle to detect. The detection of nonstationary features in the GWB could indicate the presence of some relatively bright GW sources in eccentric orbits, offering new insights into the origins of the signal. This will be particularly relevant for PTAs using observations from the next generation of radio telescopes, as they will offer high-precision measurements of GWB properties and increased sensitivity to time-dependent fluctuations.

</details>


### [103] [Probing self-interacting dark matter via gravitational-wave background from eccentric supermassive black hole mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09219)
*Chen, Mu-Chun,Tang, Yong*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该文研究星系中心超大质量黑洞周围的自相互作用暗物质能否形成密度尖峰，并印刻在超大质量黑洞双星产生的随机引力波背景谱形状中；结果表明当前脉冲星计时阵列数据对 \sigma(v)/m_{\chi}\lesssim 0.66\ \mathrm{cm}^{2}/\mathrm{g} 的截面敏感，且参数推断中必须考虑双星偏心率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质本质仍未确定，尽管已有多种天文证据；自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）作为可能候选者，有望缓解冷暗物质范式中的一些问题。作者据此希望检验星系中心超大质量黑洞周围的SIDM是否形成密度尖峰，并是否影响超大质量黑洞双星产生的随机引力波背景的谱形状。

Method: 摘要未说明。

Result: 采用改进的动力学摩擦公式并一致地演化轨道动力学，作者表明当前脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）数据对自相互作用暗物质截面 \sigma(v)/m_{\chi}\lesssim 0.66\ \mathrm{cm}^{2}/\mathrm{g} 敏感，这一敏感度与其他天体物理探针相当；作者还指出，在参数推断中纳入超大质量黑洞双星的偏心率会显著影响结果。

备注：摘要未说明。

Conclusion: 作者认为，这些发现揭示了脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）观测在探测暗物质本质方面具有广阔潜力。对于超大质量黑洞周围SIDM密度尖峰的具体物理性质、其与脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲辐射或快速射电暴（FRB）的联系，以及偏心率的定量影响，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: The nature of dark matter is still mysterious despite various astronomical evidence. As a possible candidate, self-interacting dark matter (SIDM) can potentially resolve some issues appearing in the cold dark matter paradigm. Here we investigate how SIDM around supermassive black holes (SMBHs) in galaxy centers may form a density spike and imprint in the spectrum shape of stochastic gravitational-wave background from SMBH binaries (SMBHBs). Employing a refined dynamical friction formula and consistently evolving the orbital dynamics, we demonstrate that current pulsar timing array (PTA) data are sensitive to the cross section of SIDM with <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>σ</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mi>v</mml:mi><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mo>/</mml:mo><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>m</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>χ</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.66</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>cm</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mo>/</mml:mo><mml:mi>g</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, comparable to other astrophysical probes. We also highlight the importance of including the eccentricity of SMBHBs in the parameter inference, which would affect the results significantly. Our findings reveal the promising potential of PTA observations in probing the nature of dark matter.

</details>


### [104] [Strange stars admixed with dark matter: Equiparticle model in a two fluid approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.16583)
*Marzola, Isabella,Rodrigues, Everson H.,Coelho, Anderson F.,Lourenço, Odilon*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该工作在两流体框架下研究混合费米子或玻色子暗物质的奇异星，发现存在与NASA NICER对PSR J0030+0451、PSR J0740+6620的测量及HESS J1731-347质量-半径关系相符的可能稳定奇异星构型，并在较低暗粒子质量下出现带暗物质晕的构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望探讨奇异星中混合费米子或玻色子暗物质的可能性，并检验所采用的可见物质夸克模型与暗物质模型能否给出与已有观测约束相符的稳定致密星构型。摘要未说明更具体的研究背景或知识空白。

Method: 采用两流体方法描述奇异星。可见部分使用唯象夸克模型，通过重子密度依赖性纳入夸克质量的中等介质效应，自由参数由星体物质稳定性窗口分析确定，即选择每重子能量低于铁核的参数化。暗物质部分研究费米子与玻色子两类模型：前者为自旋 1/2 暗粒子，后者为暗标量介子，二者均包含排斥矢量相互作用。摘要未说明观测数据来源之外的频率、时间跨度等细节。

Result: 结果表明可能存在稳定的奇异星构型，并与 NASA NICER 对脉冲星 PSR J0030+0451 和 PSR J0740+6620 的测量数据（含最新观测数据）以及观测到的 HESS J1731-347 质量-半径关系相一致。另一个特征是，在较低的暗粒子质量取值下，发现恒星呈现暗物质晕构型。摘要未给出具体数值或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 作者认为，混合费米子或玻色子暗物质的奇异星情景可以给出与 NICER 脉冲星测量和 HESS J1731-347 质量-半径关系相符的稳定构型，且玻色子模型中的排斥矢量相互作用有助于避免因缺乏简并压而导致的塌缩；较低暗粒子质量下可形成暗物质晕构型。摘要未讨论与脉冲星单脉冲、模式转换、计时、偏振或快速射电暴的直接联系；根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: In this work, we explore the possible scenario of strange stars admixed with fermionic or bosonic dark matter. For the description of the ``visible'' sector, we use a specific phenomenological quark model that takes into account in-medium effects for the quark masses through a suitable baryonic density dependence in which the free parameters are chosen from the analysis of the stellar matter stability window (parametrizations presenting lower energy per baryon than iron nuclei). In the case of the dark sector, we investigate the predictions of fermionic and bosonic models. In the former, we consider a spin <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:mo>/</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula> dark particle, and the latter is described by a dark scalar meson. Both models present a repulsive vector interaction, which is particularly important for the bosonic model since it helps avoid the collapse of the star due to the lack of degeneracy pressure. Our results point out possible stable strange star configurations in agreement with data from the NASA NICER measurements of the pulsars PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>0030</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0451</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> and PSR <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>0740</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>6620</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, including the latest observational data, as well as the observed HESS J1731-347 mass-radius relationship. As another feature, we find stars with dark matter halo configurations for lower values of the dark particle mass.

</details>


### [105] [Scattering of wave dark matter by supermassive black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.00090)
*Tomaselli, Giovanni Maria*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该研究从第一性原理推导出双黑洞周围随双星共转的波暗物质普适密度分布，将其解释为散射过程的稳态，并表明该散射在双星间距与暗物质德布罗意波长之比取离散值时因束缚态共振而特别有效，且可对脉冲星计时阵列谱斜率产生可观测改变。这为利用低频引力波观测探究暗物质本质提供了新可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期双黑洞周围波暗物质模拟发现了一种随双星共转的普适密度分布，但其成因尚缺乏从第一性原理出发的解释。该文旨在推导这一分布，并探究其是否对应某种散射过程的稳态，以及该过程能否在观测上留下可探测的印记。

Method: 摘要未说明具体的数值模拟设置、解析推导步骤、所采用的方程或分析方法。文中提到从第一性原理推导该密度分布，并将其解释为散射过程的稳态；考察双星间距与暗物质德布罗意波长之比取离散值时的散射效率；并估计银河系中心超大质量黑洞双星处发生此类散射的暗物质数量。

Result: 该文从第一性原理推导出随双星共转的普适密度分布，并将其解释为散射过程的稳态。散射在双星间距与暗物质德布罗意波长之比取某些离散值时变得特别有效，这可解释为束缚态共振。作者估计了银河系中心超大质量黑洞双星处经历此类散射的暗物质数量，并表明该过程能够引起脉冲星计时阵列谱斜率的可观测改变。摘要未给出具体的数值、显著性水平或定量结果。

Conclusion: 作者认为该散射过程能够对脉冲星计时阵列谱斜率产生可观测改变，从而为通过低频引力波观测洞察暗物质本质开辟了新可能性。文中对离散比值下散射效率增强给出束缚态共振的解释，但该解释的具体物理机制根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Recent simulations of wave dark matter around black hole binaries revealed the formation of a universal density profile that corotates with the binary. We derive this profile from first principles, interpreting it as the steady state of a scattering process. We find that the scattering becomes particularly efficient when the ratio of the binary separation to the dark matter's de Broglie wavelength assumes certain discrete values, which can be interpreted as bound state resonances. After estimating the amount of dark matter that undergoes this type of scattering off supermassive black hole binaries at galactic centers, we demonstrate that the process can induce an observable modification of the slope of the pulsar timing array spectrum. This opens up a new possibility to gain insights on the nature of dark matter from observations of low-frequency gravitational waves.

</details>


### [106] [Supermassive binaries in ultralight dark matter solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.16348)
*Boey, Russell,Kendall, Emily,Wang, Yourong,Easther, Richard*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 对位于大质量暗物质晕孤子中的高质量超大质量黑洞双星进行模拟后发现，其轨道衰减比先前模拟和半解析近似更快；当 ULDM 粒子质量大于 $10^{-21}\ \mathrm{eV}$ 时，可能缓解最终秒差距问题，并可能抑制脉冲星计时频段较低频率处的引力波产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻（模糊）暗物质是冷暗物质的替代方案，其关键特征为坍缩暗物质晕中心存在孤子核。孤子核可能增加超大质量黑洞双星所受阻力，改变并合动力学及由此产生的引力波背景；本文据此研究孤子中的高质量 SMBH 双星并合动力学。

Method: 摘要说明作者对大质量暗物质晕孤子中的高质量 SMBH 双星进行了详细模拟。摘要未说明具体模拟设置、数值方法、样本、时间跨度或观测手段；摘要提及与此前模拟和半解析近似进行比较，并考察 ULDM 粒子质量的影响。

Result: 模拟显示，大质量暗物质晕孤子中的高质量 SMBH 双星衰减比此前模拟和半解析近似更快。作者证实阻力强烈依赖 ULDM 粒子质量，并发现质量大于 $10^{-21}\ \mathrm{eV}$ 的 ULDM 可能缓解最终秒差距问题，且 ULDM 甚至可能抑制脉冲星计时频段较低频率处的引力波产生。

Conclusion: 作者认为，孤子核造成的阻力可改变 SMBH 双星并合动力学；大于 $10^{-21}\ \mathrm{eV}$ 的 ULDM 粒子质量可能有助于缓解最终秒差距问题，并可能抑制脉冲星计时频段较低频率处的引力波产生。摘要仅以“可能”表述这些影响；关于具体物理机制及向脉冲星、磁星或 FRB 研究的可迁移性，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Ultralight (or fuzzy) dark matter (ULDM) is an alternative to cold dark matter. A key feature of ULDM is the presence of solitonic cores at the centers of collapsed halos. These would potentially increase the drag experienced by supermassive black hole (SMBH) binaries, changing their merger dynamics and the resulting gravitational wave background. We perform detailed simulations of high-mass SMBH binaries in the soliton of a massive halo. We find more rapid decay than previous simulations and semianalytic approximations. We confirm expectations that the drag depends strongly on the ULDM particle mass, finding masses greater than <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>21</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>eV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> could potentially alleviate the final parsec problem and that ULDM may even suppress gravitational wave production at lower frequencies in the pulsar timing band.

</details>


### [107] [Constraints on an electrophilic scalar coupling from rotating magnetized stars and effects of the cosmic neutrino background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18309)
*Poddar, Tanmay Kumar,Lambiase, Gaetano*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 以 Crab 脉冲星和 PSR J0737-3039A/B 为测试案例，作者利用长程力、双星轨道周期衰减和脉冲星自转减慢率的观测，约束电亲标量与电子的耦合，其中轨道周期衰减给出最严格限制；但若该标量与宇宙中微子背景相互作用，这些约束可能显著减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻电亲标量场与磁层或恒星中恒定电子数密度耦合时，可在旋转磁化恒星周围产生长程类汤川空间分布并形成有效标量荷，进而导致双星中两颗致密星之间的长程力，并可能从孤立脉冲星或双脉冲星双星系统辐射。摘要据此以 Crab 脉冲星和 PSR J0737-3039A/B 为测试案例来约束标量-电子耦合。

Method: 摘要说明使用 Crab 脉冲星和 PSR J0737-3039A/B 作为测试案例，分析长程力、双星系统中轨道周期衰减以及脉冲星自转减慢率的观测，以推导对标量-电子耦合的约束。摘要未说明具体望远镜或仪器、观测频段、时间跨度、样本规模及统计分析细节。

Result: 摘要报告，在所分析的观测中，对标量-电子耦合最严格的限制来自双星系统的轨道周期衰减。摘要还指出，如果该标量与无处不在的宇宙中微子背景发生相互作用，这些约束可能会显著减弱。

Conclusion: 作者认为，通过提高实验灵敏度，并研究具有更强磁场和更高角速度的致密天体，可以进一步加强这些约束。摘要未给出具体数值界限；关于磁星、快速射电暴（FRB）、单脉冲发射或脉冲轮廓演化的直接联系，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: An ultralight electrophilic scalar field can produce a long-range Yukawa-like spatial profile around a rotating, magnetized star when coupled to the constant number density of electrons in either the magnetosphere or the star itself. This long-range scalar field generates an effective scalar charge in the star or its magnetosphere, leading to a long-range force between two compact stars in a binary system. The electrophilic scalar can also radiate from isolated pulsars or double pulsar binary systems. Using the Crab pulsar and PSR J0737-3039A/B as test cases, we derive constraints on the scalar-electron coupling by analyzing observations of long-range force, orbital period decay in binary systems, and pulsar spin-down rates. Among these, the most stringent limits on the coupling are obtained from the orbital period decay. However, these constraints can be significantly reduced if the scalar interacts with the pervasive cosmic neutrino background. Enhancing experimental sensitivity and studying compact objects with stronger magnetic fields and higher angular velocities could further strengthen these bounds.

</details>


### [108] [Louder gravitational wave bang from a fast-expanding universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18693)
*Brown, Lucas,Profumo, Stefano,Gangadharan, Aditi,Koç, Zeynep Su*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该文用一个简单玩具模型指出，早期快于标准速率的膨胀可提高一阶宇宙学相变所产生引力波信号的上限，从而扩大可能匹配 NANOGrav 及其他脉冲星计时阵列所报告低频引力波信号的暗扇区情景范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NANOGrav 合作组及其他脉冲星计时阵列报告了低频引力波信号，暗扇区中的强一阶相变可能是其全部或部分来源；但暗相变产生的引力波背景振幅可能不足以匹配该信号，因此需要探讨其他机制来增强这一信号。

Method: 摘要未说明。

Result: 通过一个简单的玩具模型表明，即使来自暗相变的引力波背景振幅不足以匹配 NANOGrav 信号，早期被修改的膨胀速率仍可能显著增强引力波信号；特别是当一种或多种额外能量密度源随温度以高于辐射的幂次红移、从而触发快于标准的膨胀速率时，一阶宇宙学相变引力波信号的上限会被提高，可能匹配 NANOGrav 信号的暗扇区情景范围随之扩大。

Conclusion: 作者认为，早期快于标准的膨胀速率可提高一阶宇宙学相变引力波信号的上限，从而扩大可能匹配 NANOGrav 及其他脉冲星计时阵列所报告信号的暗扇区情景范围。该论证基于一个简单的玩具模型；摘要未给出具体模型参数、数值或统计显著性，对脉冲星、磁星或快速射电暴（FRB）的物理关联，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: A strong first-order phase transition in a dark sector may produce all or part of the low-frequency gravitational wave signal recently reported by the NANOGrav Collaboration and other pulsar timing arrays. Here we point out, with a simple toy model, that even if the amplitude of the gravitational wave background from the dark phase transition is insufficient to match the NANOGrav signal, a modified expansion rate at early times may considerably enhance the gravitational wave signal. In particular, a faster-than-standard expansion rate, triggered, for instance, by the presence of one or more additional sources of energy density redshifting with higher powers of temperatures than radiation, boosts upper limits on the gravitational wave signal from first-order cosmological phase transitions, enlarging the slate of possible dark sector scenarios matching the NANOGrav signal.

</details>


### [109] [Detectability of dark matter density distribution via gravitational waves from binary black holes in the Galactic Center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19327)
*Li, Zhijin,Guo, Xiao,Cao, Zhoujian,Zhang, Yun-Long*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该文研究银河系中心大质量双黑洞系统周围暗物质环境对引力波辐射的可探测影响，结果表明在gNFW轮廓（\(\gamma=2\)、1.5、1、0.5）中只有\(\gamma=2\)产生显著可探测特征，且DM尖峰形成会增强所有gNFW斜率下的可观测波形偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗物质的基本性质仍未知，其密度轮廓存在显著不确定性。围绕大质量双黑洞的暗物质环境会改变轨道动力学并改变引力波辐射，因此银河系中心BBH系统可能成为探测暗物质密度分布的手段；但银河系中心暗物质轮廓本身不确定，需要系统考察不同轮廓以识别可探测区域。

Method: 考察广义Navarro-Frenk-White（gNFW）轮廓及其postspike修正两种情形；研究DM动力学摩擦与吸积对椭圆轨道上次级黑洞偏心率和半通径的演化效应；在质量-半通径参数空间中构建具有不同初始偏心率的轨道模型；利用来自Square Kilometer Array的30年模拟脉冲星计时阵列数据识别可探测参数区域。

Result: 在gNFW轮廓（\(\gamma=2\)、1.5、1、0.5）中，只有\(\gamma=2\)产生显著的可探测特征；DM尖峰的形成进一步增强了对所有gNFW斜率而言的这些可观测波形偏离。

Conclusion: 作者认为，银河系中心BBH系统中由暗物质动力学摩擦引起的引力波谱偏离可能被未来空间基探测器观测到，并且这些GW观测特征可用于约束不同的DM密度轮廓；在所考察的gNFW斜率中，仅\(\gamma=2\)产生显著可探测特征，而DM尖峰形成会增强所有gNFW斜率下的可观测波形偏离。关于该分析对脉冲星、磁星或快速射电暴研究的直接物理可迁移性，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: The fundamental nature of dark matter (DM) remains unknown, with significant uncertainties in its density profile. DM environments surrounding massive binary black holes (BBHs) modify their orbital dynamics, thereby altering gravitational wave (GW) emissions. For BBH systems at the Galactic Center, dynamical friction induced by DM spikes could produce detectable deviations in GW spectra, potentially observable by future space-based detectors. To address the uncertainties in the Galactic Center's DM profile, we systematically examine two scenarios: the generalized Navarro-Frenk-White (gNFW) profile and its postspike modification. We investigate the evolutionary effects of DM dynamical friction and accretion on the eccentricity and semilatus rectum of secondary black holes (BHs) in elliptical orbits. By constructing orbital models with varying initial eccentricities across the mass-semilatus rectum parameter space and utilizing 30 years of simulated pulsar timing array data from the Square Kilometer Array, we identify detectable parameter regimes of DM effects and employ these GW observational signatures to constrain different DM density profiles. Our analysis reveals that among gNFW profiles (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>γ</mml:mi><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>, 1.5, 1, 0.5), only <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>γ</mml:mi><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula> produces significant detectable signatures. The formation of DM spikes further enhances these observable waveform deviations for all gNFW slopes.

</details>


### [110] [Supersymmetric hybrid inflation and current-carrying metastable cosmic strings in <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>S</mml:mi><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>4</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>c</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:mi>S</mml:mi><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>L</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>R</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>](https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.11410)
*Afzal, Adeela,Mehmood, Maria,Rehman, Mansoor Ur,Shafi, Qaisar*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该文构建了一个基于移位 $\mu$-混合暴胀的超对称模型，其中暴胀后对称性破缺产生携带右手性中微子零模的超重亚稳态宇宙弦，其所产生的随机引力波背景与当前 PTA 数据集以及 LIGO O3 运行一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出，需要构建可在当前脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）实验中研究的超重亚稳态宇宙弦模型，并将暴胀与拓扑缺陷问题联系起来：所构造的对称性破缺需沿暴胀轨迹进行以使拓扑不稳定的原初磁单极子被暴胀消去。

Method: 摘要描述了构建基于移位 $\mu$-混合暴胀的超对称模型，其中对称性破缺 $SU(4)_c \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_R \rightarrow SU(3)_c \times SU(2)_L \times U(1)_{B-L} \times U(1)_R$ 沿暴胀轨迹发生，暴胀结束后 $U(1)_{B-L} \times U(1)_R \rightarrow U(1)_Y$ 破缺产生超重亚稳态宇宙弦。所得随机引力波背景与当前 PTA 数据集及 LIGO O3 运行进行比对，并计算标量谱指数 $n_s$ 与张量标量比 $r$ 与 Planck 2018 和 Atacama Cosmology Telescope 测量作比较。摘要未说明具体分析技术细节。

Result: 暴胀结束后 $U(1)_{B-L} \times U(1)_R \rightarrow U(1)_Y$ 的破缺产生携带右手性中微子零模的超重亚稳态宇宙弦，这些零模产生随机引力波背景，摘要称其与当前 PTA 数据集以及 LIGO O3 运行一致。标量谱指数 $n_s$ 和张量标量比 $r$ 与 Planck 2018 和 Atacama Cosmology Telescope 测量相容。摘要未给出具体数值或显著性。

Conclusion: 作者认为该超对称移位 $\mu$-混合暴胀模型可产生超重亚稳态宇宙弦，其随机引力波背景与当前 PTA 数据集及 LIGO O3 运行一致，且 $n_s$ 与 $r$ 与 Planck 2018 和 Atacama Cosmology Telescope 测量相容；摘要还简要讨论了重加热和轻子生成。该工作未在摘要中直接涉及脉冲星单脉冲、脉冲轮廓演化、模式转换、计时、偏振、射电谱或磁星与 FRB 的联系，根据摘要无法判断

Abstract: We construct a realistic supersymmetric model for superheavy metastable cosmic strings (CSs) that can be investigated in the current pulsar timing array (PTA) experiments. It is based on shifted <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>μ</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>-hybrid inflation in which the symmetry breaking <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>S</mml:mi><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>4</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>c</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:mi>S</mml:mi><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>L</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>R</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>--&gt;</mml:mo><mml:mi>S</mml:mi><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>3</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>c</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:mi>S</mml:mi><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>L</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mrow><mml:mi>B</mml:mi><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mi>L</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>R</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> proceeds along an inflationary trajectory such that the topologically unstable primordial monopoles are inflated away. The breaking of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mrow><mml:mi>B</mml:mi><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mi>L</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>R</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>--&gt;</mml:mo><mml:mi>U</mml:mi><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>1</mml:mn><mml:msub><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>Y</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> after inflation ends yields the superheavy metastable CSs carrying the right handed neutrino zero modes that generate the stochastic gravitational wave background, which we show is consistent with the current PTA dataset and the LIGO O3 run. The scalar spectral index <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mi>s</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> and the tensor-to-scalar ratio <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>r</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula> are compatible with the Planck 2018 and Atacama Cosmology Telescope measurements. Both reheating and leptogenesis are briefly discussed.

</details>


### [111] [Gravitational wave propagation beyond general relativity: Geometric optic expansion and lens-induced dispersion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.07164)
*Menadeo, Nicola,Zumalacárregui, Miguel*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 本文在短波展开中建立了超越广义相对论的引力波传播理论，首次给出领头阶振幅和相位的修正，并以 Brans-Dicke 理论为例计算了所有度规和标量场扰动的色散修正；但结论指出，在与太阳系和双脉冲星限制相容的 Brans-Dicke 理论中，引力波透镜效应太小而无法观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波传播检验可约束超越广义相对论的引力理论，因为微小偏离会在宇宙学距离上累积；但此类检验受限于平均宇宙学时空的高对称性。偏离均匀性即引力透镜可引入新相互作用，例如标准引力波偏振与不同自旋的新标量场或矢量场之间的相互作用，因此超越广义相对论的引力波透镜提供了检验宇宙学引力的新途径。

Method: 摘要说明本文提出短波展开下超越广义相对论的引力波传播理论，并首次包含对领头阶振幅和相位的修正；作为示例，计算了 Brans-Dicke 理论中所有度规和标量场扰动的色散（频率相关）修正，该理论被描述为展现引力波色散的最简单修改理论。摘要未说明具体观测、仪器、频段、时间跨度或样本。

Result: 摘要报告建立了短波展开中超越广义相对论的引力波传播理论，并首次包含对领头阶振幅和相位的修正；作为例子，计算了 Brans-Dicke 中所有度规和标量场扰动的色散（频率相关）修正。摘要还指出，在与太阳系和双脉冲星限制相容的 Brans-Dicke 理论中，引力波透镜效应太小而无法观测。摘要未给出具体数值或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 作者明确指出，在与太阳系和双脉冲星限制相容的 Brans-Dicke 理论中，引力波透镜效应太小而无法观测。该形式体系开启了新颖引力检验的可能性，包括暗能量理论和屏蔽机制。与脉冲星、磁星、快速射电暴或单脉冲观测的直接联系，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: The nature of gravity can be tested by how gravitational waves (GWs) are emitted, detected, and propagate through the universe. Propagation tests are powerful, as small deviations compound over cosmological distances. However, GW propagation tests of theories beyond Einstein's general relativity (GR) are limited by the high degree of symmetry of the average cosmological spacetime. Deviations from homogeneity, i.e. gravitational lenses, allow for new interactions, e.g. between standard GW polarization and new scalar or vector fields, with different spin. Therefore, GW lensing beyond GR offers novel tests of cosmological gravity. Here we present the theory of GW propagation beyond GR in the short-wave expansion, including corrections to the leading-order amplitude and phase for the first time. As an example, we compute the dispersive (frequency-dependent) corrections to all metric and scalar field perturbations in Brans-Dicke, the simplest modified theory exhibiting GW dispersion. GW lensing effects are too small to observe in Brans-Dicke theories compatible with solar system and binary pulsar limits. Nevertheless, our formalism opens the possibility of novel tests of gravity, including dark-energy theories and screening mechanisms.

</details>


### [112] [Cosmological constraints on small-scale primordial non-Gaussianity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22614)
*Zhou, Jing-Zhi,Li, Zhi-Chao,Wu, Di*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该文结合 PTA、CMB、BAO、PBH 丰度观测与 LISA 信噪比分析，限制小尺度原初功率谱 P_ζ(k) 和局部型原初非高斯性 f_NL 的参数空间；结果表明单色原初功率谱产生的标量诱导引力波更可能主导当前 PTA 观测，并给出 -10.0 < f_NL < 1.2 的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大尺度原初功率谱 P_ζ(k) 与原初非高斯性 f_NL 已被严格限制，但小尺度 P_ζ(k) 与 f_NL 所受限制相对较弱，因此需要在局部型原初非高斯性框架下研究小尺度大振幅功率谱及其可观测后果。

Method: 摘要说明作者考虑局部型原初非高斯性，研究大振幅小尺度原初功率谱产生的原初黑洞与标量诱导引力波；所用数据包括 PTA、CMB、BAO 观测数据以及 PBH 丰度，并结合 LISA 的信噪比分析；还考察不同形状的原初功率谱对限制的影响并计算各模型的贝叶斯因子。具体观测频段、时间跨度、样本量与统计方法摘要未说明。

Result: 摘要报告作者严格限制了 P_ζ(k) 与 f_NL 的参数空间，并计算了不同原初功率谱形状下的贝叶斯因子。结果指示，单色原初功率谱产生的 SIGWs 更可能主导当前 PTA 观测，对应的原初非高斯参数限制为 -10.0 < f_NL < 1.2。

Conclusion: 作者认为，在当前所用观测数据与信噪比分析下，单色原初功率谱产生的标量诱导引力波更可能主导当前 PTA 观测，并据此给出原初非高斯参数的限制；至于该结果对其他小尺度原初非高斯模型或脉冲星、磁星、快速射电暴等具体物理过程的可迁移性，摘要未说明。

Abstract: In contrast to the large-scale primordial power spectrum <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>P</mml:mi><mml:mi>ζ</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mi>k</mml:mi><mml:mo>)</mml:mo></mml:math></inline-formula> and primordial non-Gaussianity <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>f</mml:mi><mml:mi>NL</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>, which are strictly constrained, the small-scale <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>P</mml:mi><mml:mi>ζ</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mi>k</mml:mi><mml:mo>)</mml:mo></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>f</mml:mi><mml:mi>NL</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> are not as limited. Considering local-type primordial non-Gaussianity, we study the primordial black holes (PBHs) and scalar induced gravitational waves (SIGWs) caused by large-amplitude small-scale primordial power spectrum. By analyzing current observational data from pulsar timing array (PTA), cosmic microwave background (CMB), baryon acoustic oscillations (BAO), and abundance of PBHs, and combining it with the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) analysis of Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA), we rigorously constrain the parameter space of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>P</mml:mi><mml:mi>ζ</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mi>k</mml:mi><mml:mo>)</mml:mo></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>f</mml:mi><mml:mi>NL</mml:mi></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>. Furthermore, we examine the effects of different shapes of the primordial power spectrum on these constraints and comprehensively calculate the Bayesian factors for various models. Our results indicate that SIGWs generated by a monochromatic primordial power spectrum are more likely to dominate current PTA observations, with the corresponding constraint on the primordial non-Gaussian parameter being <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>10.0</mml:mn><mml:mo>&lt;</mml:mo><mml:msub><mml:mi>f</mml:mi><mml:mi>NL</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>&lt;</mml:mo><mml:mn>1.2</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>.

</details>


### [113] [Attempt to study axion-photon coupling using compact binary systems with high Shapiro time delay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.19233)
*Wang, Zhao-Yang,Tian, Hao-Chen,Liang, Yun-Feng*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该工作建立并数值求解了高 Shapiro 时间延迟脉冲星双星系统中轴子-光子转换及沿路径磁场的模型，针对 PSR J1614-2230 和 PSR J1910-5959A 得到的轴子/ALP 参数上限弱于已有约束，但作者认为方法更可靠、仍有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望利用脉冲星双星系统研究轴子-光子转换。此类系统中轨道平面几乎平行于视线方向，当伴星位于脉冲星与地球之间时，脉冲星辐射束会穿过伴星磁场，可能发生轴子-光子转换；作者指出，用这类系统研究轴子或类轴子粒子（ALP）的优势是源光子的内禀强度和偏振态可以被很好地确定。

Method: 摘要说明作者分析了轴子-光子转换以及沿光子路径的磁场，建立相应模型并数值求解；选取 PSR J1614-2230 和 PSR J1910-5959A 作为研究对象，并假设伴星的磁场、磁轴角和探测器灵敏度，以讨论利用高 Shapiro 延迟双星系统限制轴子/ALP 参数空间的可行性。观测频段、时间跨度、样本量与统计分析方法：摘要未说明。

Result: 摘要报告，在轨道平面几乎平行于视线的双星系统中，当伴星位于脉冲星与地球之间时，脉冲星辐射束穿过伴星磁场可能发生轴子-光子转换，从而影响光子的强度和线偏振。作者建立了相应模型并数值求解，针对 PSR J1614-2230 和 PSR J1910-5959A 进行讨论，发现由这两个源得到的上限弱于已有约束。具体数值、置信水平与统计显著性：摘要未说明。

Conclusion: 作者认为，虽然由 PSR J1614-2230 和 PSR J1910-5959A 得到的轴子/ALP 参数上限弱于已有约束，但考虑到其更高的可靠性，本工作提出的方法仍有价值；随着更多合适样本和更丰富观测结果被发现，对轴子参数的进一步限制可以得到加强。根据摘要无法判断该方法对脉冲星、磁星、快速射电暴（FRB）等其他主题的可迁移性。

Abstract: We study the axion-photon conversion process in pulsar binary systems with high Shapiro time delay. In these binary systems, the orbital plane is nearly parallel to the line of sight. When the companion star is positioned between the pulsar and the Earth, the pulsar radiation beam will pass through the companion's magnetic field, potentially leading to axion-photon conversion that affects the intensity and linear polarization of the photons. The advantage of using such systems for axion or axionlike particle (ALP) research is that the intrinsic intensity and polarization state of the source photons can be well determined. This work analyzes the axion-photon conversion and the magnetic field along the photon path, establishing the corresponding model and solving it numerically. We choose PSR J1614-2230 and PSR J1910-5959A for our study. By assuming the companion's magnetic field, magnetic axis angle, and detector sensitivity, we discuss the feasibility of using high Shapiro delay binary systems to constrain the axion/ALP parameter space. We find that the upper limits derived from these two sources are weaker then existing constraints. However, considering the higher reliability, the method proposed in this work is still valuable. As more suitable samples and richer observational results are discovered, further constraints on the axion parameters could be strengthened.

</details>


### [114] [Likelihoods for stochastic gravitational wave background data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.24695)
*Franciolini, Gabriele,Pieroni, Mauro,Ricciardone, Angelo,Romano, Joseph D.*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该文系统研究SGWB搜寻中的似然函数，表明用高斯替代方案近似精确Whittle似然会在SGWB参数估计中引入系统性偏差，某些时段长度和带宽下（尤其在空间基和脉冲星计时阵列中）偏差可超过统计不确定度，并为时段选择、似然选择和数据压缩提供实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGWB搜寻常将数据划分为许多短时段，并借助中心极限定理采用高斯互相关似然。摘要指出需要系统研究此类似然函数，明确用高斯替代方案近似精确Whittle似然是否会带来参数估计偏差，以及高斯性在哪些区间失效。

Method: 摘要未说明具体的观测数据、仪器、频率或频段、时间跨度和样本。作者采用由简到繁的层级化算例：从单个频率bin和单个探测器开始，扩展到具有白噪声与有色噪声及信号的2个和3个探测器；通过将精确Whittle似然与多种高斯替代方案近似进行比较，推导全似然的若干近似，并讨论以基准噪声估计为条件对全似然进行条件化的可能性。

Result: 摘要报告，用各种高斯替代方案近似精确Whittle似然，会在SGWB参数估计中引入系统性偏差；作者识别出高斯性失效的区间，并给出全似然的若干近似。摘要还指出，对于某些时段长度和带宽，特别是在空间基和脉冲星计时阵列中，偏差可能超过统计不确定度。

Conclusion: 作者认为，这些结果为时段选择、似然选择和数据压缩策略提供了实用指导，以确保当前和下一代引力波探测器中进行稳健的SGWB推断；并讨论了将全似然以基准噪声估计为条件以获得无偏SGWB参数估计的可能性。关于与脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、模式转换、射电偏振或FRB的具体物理联系，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of likelihood functions used for stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB) searches. By dividing the data into many short segments, one customarily takes advantage of the central limit theorem to justify a Gaussian cross-correlation likelihood. We show, with a hierarchy of ever more realistic examples---beginning with a single frequency bin and one detector, and then moving to two and three detectors with white and colored signal and noise---that approximating the exact Whittle likelihood by various Gaussian alternatives can induce systematic biases in the estimation of the SGWB parameters. We derive several approximations for the full likelihood and identify regimes where Gaussianity breaks down. We also discuss the possibility of conditioning the full likelihood on fiducial noise estimates to produce unbiased SGWB parameter estimation. We show that for some segment durations and bandwidths, particularly in space-based and pulsar-timing arrays, the bias can exceed the statistical uncertainty. Our results provide practical guidance for segment choice, likelihood selection, and data-compression strategies to ensure robust SGWB inference in current and next-generation gravitational wave detectors.

</details>


### [115] [Origin of the stochastic gravitational wave background: First-order phase transition versus black hole mergers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.13729)
*Winkler, Martin Wolfgang,Freese, Katherine*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 对 NANOGrav、Parkes 和 European Pulsar Timing Array 数据集的频率派假设检验显示，观测到的 nHz 随机引力波谱相较于黑洞并合模型，以 $2-3\sigma$ 的显著性更偏好一阶相变解释；最佳拟合为气泡碰撞主导的相变，其峰值频率略高于 PTA 测量范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NANOGrav、Parkes 和 European Pulsar Timing Array 实验已在 nHz 频段收集到随机引力波背景的强有力证据，其物理起源尚待阐明。作者要检验标准解释（超大质量黑洞并合）与突出替代解释（一阶相变）哪一个更符合观测信号。

Method: 对 PTA 信号进行详细的统计分析，并采用频率派假设检验，将超大质量黑洞并合解释与一阶相变解释进行对比；对不同黑洞模型分别检验，并给出相变产生引力波的不同机制（气泡碰撞与声波）的拟合。摘要未说明所用具体数据集细节、频率范围数值、时间跨度或样本量。

Result: 观测到的引力波谱相较于黑洞并合更偏好一阶相变，显著性为 $2-3\sigma$，具体取决于所采用的黑洞模型。这一轻微偏好与观测信号振幅相对较大（高于黑洞模型的典型预期）以及其谱形有关，后者略微更有利于相变谱而非黑洞模型中占主导的单一幂律谱。对组合 PTA 数据集的最佳拟合为一种主要通过气泡碰撞而非声波产生引力波信号的相变；最佳拟合能量密度谱在 PTA 频段内呈现从陡峭上升幂律（因果性尾部）到平缓上升幂律的转折，峰值频率略高于 PTA 测量范围。

Conclusion: 作者认为，该谱可由真空隧穿厚壁机制中的强一阶相变获得，该相变将宇宙再加热至 $T_*\sim \mathrm{GeV}$；GeV 能标下的暗部门相变也提供相当好的拟合。因此，一阶相变被提出为随机引力波背景物理起源的有力替代解释。

Abstract: The NANOGrav, Parkes and European Pulsar Timing Array (PTA) experiments have collected strong evidence for a stochastic gravitational wave background in the nHz-frequency band. In this work we perform a detailed statistical analysis of the signal in order to elucidate its physical origin. Specifically, we test the standard explanation in terms of supermassive black hole mergers against the prominent alternative explanation in terms of a first-order phase transition. By means of a frequentist hypothesis test we find that the observed gravitational wave spectrum prefers a first-order phase transition at <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>3</mml:mn><mml:mi>σ</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> significance compared to black hole mergers (depending on the underlying black hole model). This mild preference is linked to the relatively large amplitude of the observed gravitational wave signal (above the typical expectation of black hole models) and to its spectral shape (which slightly favors the phase-transition spectrum over the predominantly single power-law spectrum predicted in black hole models). The best fit to the combined PTA dataset is obtained for a phase transition which dominantly produces the gravitational wave signal by bubble collisions (rather than by sound waves). The best-fit (energy-density) spectrum features, within the frequency band of the PTA experiments, a crossover from a steeply rising power law (causality tail) to a softly rising power law; the peak frequency then falls slightly above the PTA-measured range. Such a spectrum can be obtained for a strong first-order phase transition in the thick-wall regime of vacuum tunneling which reheats the Universe to a temperature of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>T</mml:mi><mml:mo>*</mml:mo></mml:msub><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:mi>GeV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>. A dark sector phase transition at the GeV-scale provides a comparably good fit.

</details>


### [116] [Connecting inflation to the NANOGrav 15-year dataset via massive gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.09658)
*Kenjale, Ved,Kahniashvili, Tina*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 在时间依赖的最小大质量引力理论框架下，用暴胀解释NANOGrav 15年数据集中的随机引力波背景需要强蓝谱 \(n_T = 1.8 \pm 0.54\) 和约 \(4000\ \mathrm{GeV}\) 的低重加热能标；大质量引力允许比广义相对论更高的重加热温度，使暴胀解释略微不那么困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个PTA项目在最新数据集中报告了令人信服的随机引力波背景证据，其起源可能是天体物理的，也可能存在多种宇宙学起源。作者针对与其他PTA结果符合良好的NANOGrav信号，考察暴胀作为该背景来源的可能性，并在标准广义相对论之外引入时间依赖的最小大质量引力理论进行分析。

Method: 摘要未说明具体的观测或数据处理细节，仅表明分析聚焦于NANOGrav信号（该信号与其他PTA项目符合良好），并在时间依赖的最小大质量引力理论、而非标准广义相对论的框架下评估暴胀作为随机引力波背景来源的可能性，同时考虑大爆炸核合成限制对重加热能标的约束。

Result: 研究发现，用暴胀解释该背景需要一个强蓝谱，其特征为 \(n_T = 1.8 \pm 0.54\)；大爆炸核合成限制则要求较低的重加热能标 \(T_{\rm rh} \simeq 4000\ \mathrm{GeV}\)。这些限制使暴胀难以成为NANOGrav信号的来源，但大质量引力模型允许比标准广义相对论更高的重加热温度。

Conclusion: 作者认为，上述约束使暴胀难以成为NANOGrav信号的来源；不过，由于大质量引力模型允许比标准广义相对论更高的重加热温度，暴胀解释因此略微不那么困难。该结论涉及的是暴胀起源与引力理论框架，摘要未说明其与脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、FRB等主题的直接联系，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Several pulsar timing array (PTA) missions have reported convincing evidence of a stochastic gravitational wave background within their latest datasets. This background could originate from an astrophysical source, though there are multiple possibilities for its origin to be cosmological. Focusing on the North American Nanohertz Observatory for Gravitational Waves (NANOGrav) signal, which was in good agreement with other PTAs, we evaluate the possibility of an inflationary source for the background. However, we will consider a time-dependent minimal theory of massive gravity instead of standard general relativity for our analysis. We find that an inflationary interpretation will require a strongly blue spectrum, characterized by <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mi>T</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>1.8</mml:mn><mml:mo>±</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.54</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>, while big bang nucleosynthesis limits will require a low reheating scale of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>T</mml:mi><mml:mi>rh</mml:mi></mml:msub><mml:mo>≃</mml:mo><mml:mn>4000</mml:mn><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>GeV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>. Although these constraints make it difficult for inflation to be the source of the NANOGrav signal, we find that the massive gravity model allows for a greater reheating temperature than standard general relativity, making an inflationary interpretation slightly less cumbersome.

</details>


### [117] [Astrophysical systematics on testing general relativity with gravitational waves from Galactic double white dwarfs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17418)
*Lau, Shu Yan,Yagi, Kent,Arras, Phil*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该工作表明，用LISA探测的银河系双白矮星检验引力时，若灵敏度达到可与脉冲星观测现有约束相当的水平，天体物理效应（潮汐同步、磁单极感应、四极形变）引起的系统误差将主导统计误差，因此必须在波形模型中予以考虑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波为强场区域超越GR的引力理论提供了新限制，LISA预期的银河系双白矮星信号也有潜力限制在较不致密双星中产生较大偏离GR的理论（如标量辐射）；但近距双白矮星的轨道演化受恒星自转、潮汐和磁相互作用等天体物理效应影响，使引力检验复杂化。

Method: 采用参数化后爱因斯坦模型刻画信号中领先的超越GR效应，并使用Fisher信息矩阵估计测量不确定度；随后研究忽略恒星自转、潮汐相互作用、磁相互作用等每一种天体物理效应在参数估计中造成的系统误差。摘要未说明具体观测数据、望远镜或频段。

Result: 数值结果表明，要给出与脉冲星观测现有约束相当的非GR效应限制，需要双星质量的严格先验和较长观测时间；在该灵敏度水平下，天体物理效应造成的系统误差主导统计误差。最显著的效应是足够大磁场（>10^7 G）下潮汐同步与磁单极感应的力矩；即使较弱的四极形变效应也与统计不确定度同量级，不能在波形模型中忽略。

Conclusion: 作者明确结论是：在使用LISA探测到的银河系双白矮星检验引力时，必须在参数估计中仔细计入天体物理效应。就与脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、FRB等主题的联系而言，摘要仅将脉冲星观测作为现有非GR限制的比较基准，未涉及磁星、单脉冲、FRB或计时相关分析；其他物理可迁移性根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Gravitational waves have been shown to provide new constraints on gravitational theories beyond general relativity (GR), especially in the strong-field regime. Gravitational-wave signals from Galactic double white dwarfs, expected to be detected by the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA), also have the potential to place stringent bounds on certain theories that give rise to relatively large deviations from GR in less compact binaries, such as through scalar radiation. Nevertheless, the orbital evolution of close double white dwarf systems is also affected by various astrophysical effects, such as stellar rotation, tidal interactions, and magnetic interactions, which add complexity to the gravity tests. In this work, we employ the parametrized post-Einsteinian model to capture the leading beyond-GR effect on the signal and estimate the measurement uncertainties using the Fisher information matrix. We then study the systematic error caused by ignoring each astrophysical effect mentioned above on the parameter estimation. Our numerical results show that, to place bounds on the non-GR effects comparable to existing bounds from pulsar observations, tight priors on the mass of the binary and long observation time are required. At this level of sensitivity, we found that systematic errors from the astrophysical effects dominate statistical errors. The most significant effects investigated here are torques from tidal synchronization and magnetic unipolar induction for sufficiently large magnetic fields (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>&gt;</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mn>7</mml:mn></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>G</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>). Meanwhile, even the weaker astrophysical effects from quadrupolar deformations are of a similar order of magnitude as the statistical uncertainty and, hence, cannot be ignored in the waveform model. We conclude that the astrophysical effects must be carefully accounted for in the parameter estimation to test gravity with Galactic double white dwarfs detected by LISA.

</details>


### [118] [Reconstructing primordial black hole power spectra from gravitational waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.06547)
*Frolovsky, Daniel,Kühnel, Florian,Stamou, Ioanna*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分析引力波能量密度与原初功率谱关系的非贝叶斯新方法，并利用当前脉冲星计时阵列数据，在原初黑洞形成的多种情景下实现了功率谱的系统性重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文关注引力波能量密度与原初功率谱之间的关系，尤其是标量诱导引力辐射以及原初黑洞形成的多种情景。其知识缺口在于传统贝叶斯方法在引力波研究中处理复杂数据时存在局限，需要一种更直接且计算高效的分析途径。

Method: 摘要未说明具体观测、数据、仪器、频率或频段、时间跨度与样本细节。所述方法为一种新的分析框架，用于研究引力波能量密度与原初功率谱之间的关系；该方法聚焦标量诱导引力辐射，并应用于若干原初黑洞形成情景。其特点在于区别于传统贝叶斯方法，强调直接性与计算效率，并用于处理引力波研究的复杂数据。摘要提及利用当前脉冲星计时阵列数据开展应用，但未给出具体分析步骤。

Result: 摘要未说明具体数值、显著性、趋势或事件。摘要明确指出，该方法被应用于若干原初黑洞形成情景，并证明能够利用当前脉冲星计时阵列数据对所有情景下的功率谱进行系统性重构，从而给出了其在引力波分析中潜力的清晰示例。

Conclusion: 摘要指出，该方法相较于传统贝叶斯方法具有直接性和计算效率的优势；利用当前脉冲星计时阵列数据，它能够在所有情景下系统性重构功率谱，显示出在引力波分析中的潜力。关于这些结果对脉冲星、磁星或快速射电暴的具体物理意义，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: A novel methodology for analyzing the relation between the energy density in gravitational waves and primordial power spectra is developed. Focusing on scalar-induced gravitational radiation, this methodology is applied to a number of scenarios for the primordial black hole formation. Being differed from conventional Bayesian approaches, its advantages include directness and computational efficiency, which are crucial for handling the complex data characteristic of gravitational wave research. As an important application, it is demonstrated that this methodology allows for the systematic reconstruction of power spectra across all scenarios using current pulsar timing array data, providing a clear example of its potential in gravitational wave analysis.

</details>


### [119] [Stochastic gravitational wave background from chiral superconducting cosmic strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17154)
*Rybak, I. Yu.,Sousa, L.*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该文首次在计算手征超导宇宙弦网络产生的随机引力波背景时纳入矢量辐射影响，发现强耦合可能大幅抑制引力波信号，而中等耦合下超导性可能有助于使引力波谱与脉冲星计时阵列数据相协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导宇宙弦环会发射矢量辐射，但此前对超导宇宙弦网络随机引力波背景的计算未纳入矢量辐射的影响；需要刻画矢量辐射发射效率及其对弦环演化的作用，并评估其对引力波信号的后果。

Method: 摘要指出研究超导宇宙弦环的矢量辐射发射，推导刻画矢量辐射发射效率的一般关系，研究其对弦环演化的影响；在此基础上计算手征超导宇宙弦网络产生的随机引力波背景，并首次纳入矢量辐射的影响。摘要未说明具体观测、仪器、频率、时间跨度、样本或统计方法。

Abstract: We investigate the emission of vector radiation by superconducting cosmic string loops, deriving general relations to characterize the vector radiation emission efficiency, and study its impact on the evolution of loops. Building on these results, we compute the stochastic gravitational wave background generated by a chiral superconducting cosmic string network, including the impact of vector radiation for the very first time. Our analysis reveals that strong coupling between superconducting cosmic strings and the vector field may lead to a substantial suppression of the gravitational wave signal, while moderate coupling may still produce a detectable signal. We demonstrate that, in this intermediate limit, the presence of superconductivity in cosmic strings may help reconcile their gravitational wave spectrum with pulsar timing array data for large enough values of current.

Abstract: The formation of primordial black holes or other dark matter relics from amplified density fluctuations in the early Universe may also generate scalar-induced gravitational waves (GW), carrying vital information about the primordial power spectrum and the early expansion history of our Universe. We present a Bayesian approach aimed at reconstructing both the shape of the scalar power spectrum and the Universe's equation of state from GW observations, using interpolating splines to flexibly capture features in the GW data. The optimal number of spline nodes is chosen via Bayesian evidence, aiming at balancing complexity of the model and the fidelity of the reconstruction. We test our method using both representative mock data and recent pulsar timing array measurements, demonstrating that it can accurately reconstruct the curvature power spectrum as well as the underlying equation of state, if different from radiation.

Abstract: The detection of gravitational waves (GWs) by LIGO-Virgo and pulsar timing arrays has opened a new window into early Universe cosmology. Yet, the origin of large-scale magnetic fields and the dynamics of the reheating epoch remain poorly understood. In this work, we study the generation of secondary GWs (SGWs) sourced by primordial magnetic fields produced via a Sawtooth-type coupling during reheating with a general background evolution. We show that the reheating equation of state significantly influences the spectral shape and amplitude of the magnetic fields. While a scale-invariant spectrum is typically needed to match observational bounds, this coupling naturally produces a strongly blue-tilted spectrum that remains consistent with current constraints. Crucially, the magnetic field continues to grow during reheating, leading to a GW signal with a broken power-law spectrum and a distinctive blue tilt on superhorizon scales. This SGW signal can fall within the sensitivity of upcoming detectors such as LISA, DECIGO, and BBO. The unique spectral features make this scenario distinguishable from other sources, offering a viable mechanism for cosmic magnetogenesis and a novel probe of the reheating era through GW observations.

Abstract: The gravitational-wave (GW) memory effect is a strong-field relativistic phenomenon that is associated with a persistent change in the GW strain after the passage of a GW. The nonlinear effect arises from interactions of GWs themselves in the wave zone and is an observable effect connected to the infrared properties of general relativity. The detection of the GW memory effect is possible with LIGO and Virgo in a population of binary-black-hole (BBH) mergers or from individual events with next-generation ground- and space-based GW detectors or pulsar timing arrays. Matched-filtering-based searches for the GW memory require accurate, and preferably rapid-to-evaluate waveform models of the memory effect's GW signal. One important element of such a waveform model is a model for the final memory offset---namely, the net change in strain between early and late times. In this paper, we construct a model for the final memory offset from the merger of nonspinning BBH systems in quasicircular orbits. A novel ingredient of this model is that we first compute the memory signal for extreme mass-ratio inspirals using a high post-Newtonian-order analytic calculation, and we use this analytical result to fix the coefficient in the fit which is linear in the mass-ratio. The resulting memory-offset fit could be used for detecting the GW memory for binaries that merge on a timescale that is short relative to the inverse of the low-frequency cutoff of a GW detector. Additionally, this fit will be useful for analytic waveform models of the GW memory signals in the time and frequency domains.

Abstract: In this work, we test for gravitational parity violation in the PSR J1141-6545 system by analyzing the orbital plane inclination precession induced by the misalignment between the white dwarf's spin axis and the system's total angular momentum. Using the parity-violating metric of gravity that incorporates terms from both the exterior and boundary of the field source, we calculated corrections to the relative acceleration and orbital inclination precession rates, which exhibit significant deviations from the general relativity (GR) prediction. The parity-violating contributions depend on the projection of the spin vector along the orbital angular momentum direction, contrasting with GR, where it depends on the projection within the orbital plane. The corrections are perpendicular to GR contribution, highlighting a fundamental distinction. The exterior field correction is linear in the theoretical parameter and coupled to eccentricity <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>e</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>, while the boundary term correction is quadratic. By comparing these corrections with GR and incorporating observational uncertainty, we derive the constraint on the theoretical parameter, yielding <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mover><mml:mi>f</mml:mi><mml:mo>˙</mml:mo></mml:mover><mml:mrow><mml:mi>PV</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>≲</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mn>6</mml:mn></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>m</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>.

TL;DR: 本文（系列工作第二部分）研究超大质量Kerr黑洞附近磁层中振荡“间隙”产生轴子的物理过程，并得出在合理参数下，活动星系核和类星体中的轴子发射能够显著为其晕提供轴子暗物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工作需要呈现超大质量Kerr黑洞附近产生轴子粒子所必需的物理过程。摘要指出，本文是系列工作的第二部分；其框架涉及广义相对论磁层物理，包含喷流发射的Blandford-Znajek机制以及适用于Kerr黑洞磁层的Goldreich-Julian电荷产生版本，并由此形成类似脉冲星磁层中的“间隙”。摘要未说明更具体的知识空白或研究问题背景。
