Result: 靠近视界产生的间隙源于强极向电场分量的产生，使得 <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mover><mml:mi>E</mml:mi><mml:mo>--&gt;</mml:mo></mml:mover><mml:mo>·</mml:mo><mml:mover><mml:mi>B</mml:mi><mml:mo>--&gt;</mml:mo></mml:mover><mml:mo>!=</mml:mo><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>。这些间隙是稳定的或振荡的，而新轴子的产生源于振荡的间隙。高能辐射在确定振荡间隙的参数和轴子发射功率方面起着至关重要的作用。在合理参数下，发现活动星系核和类星体中的轴子发射能够显著为其晕提供轴子暗物质。

Conclusion: 作者认为，在合理参数下，活动星系核和类星体中的轴子发射能够显著为其晕提供轴子暗物质。关于该机制与脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲发射、脉冲轮廓演化、模式转换、计时、自转减慢力矩变化、偏振、射电频谱、瞬变射电发射或磁星与快速射电暴（FRB）之间联系的适用性或可迁移性，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: This work is devoted to the production of axion particles in the vicinity of supermassive Kerr black holes, and this article is the second part of our work presenting the physical processes necessary for the production of axions. The framework is the physics of the magnetosphere in general relativity that contains the Blandford-Znajek mechanism of jet launching supplemented by a version of the Goldreich-Julian charge generation adapted to the magnetosphere of a Kerr black hole, which leads to the formation of ``gaps'' like in a pulsar magnetosphere. These gaps produced close to the horizon result from the generation of an intense poloidal component of the electric field such that <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mover><mml:mi>E</mml:mi><mml:mo>--&gt;</mml:mo></mml:mover><mml:mo>·</mml:mo><mml:mover><mml:mi>B</mml:mi><mml:mo>--&gt;</mml:mo></mml:mover><mml:mo>!=</mml:mo><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>, which is necessary for the production of axion particles. These gaps are steady or oscillating and the creation of new axions stems from oscillating gaps, which are described in order to reveal their appropriate characteristics for this purpose. High-energy radiation plays an essential role in determining the parameters of the oscillating gaps and the power of axion emission. With reasonable parameters, it is found that the axion emission in active galactic nuclei and quasars can significantly feed their halos with axion dark matter.

Abstract: Spin-2 ultralight dark matter (ULDM) is a viable dark matter candidate and it can be constrained using gravitational wave (GW) observations. In this paper, we investigate the detectability of spin-2 ULDM by space-based GW interferometers. By considering a direct coupling between spin-2 ULDM and ordinary matter, we derive the corresponding response functions and sensitivity curves for various time-delay interferometry channels and calculate the optimal sensitivity curves for future millihertz GW detectors. Our results demonstrate that the space-based detectors can place stringent constraints on the coupling constant of spin-2 ULDM, reaching <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>α</mml:mi><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> around a mass of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>m</mml:mi><mml:mo>~</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mn>10</mml:mn><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>17</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>eV</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, surpassing current limits from ground-based detectors and pulsar timing arrays. Thus, the space-based GW detectors can serve as powerful tools not only for detecting GWs but also for probing fundamental properties of ultralight dark matter.

Abstract: The Thomas-Whitehead (TW) action embeds the projectively invariant connection into a gravitational theory that contains general relativity [S. Brensinger, K. Heitritter, V. G. J. Rodgers, and K. Stiffler, [Phys. Rev. D 103, 044060 (2021)PRVDAQ2470-001010.1103/PhysRevD.103.044060]; S. Brensinger and V. G. J. Rodgers, Int. J. Mod. Phys. A 33, 1850223 (2018)IMPAEF0217-751X10.1142/S0217751X18502238; V. G. J. Rodgers, in Particles, Fields and Topology, Chap. 19, pp. 201--211]. In this work, the diffeomorphism field <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>D</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mi>a</mml:mi><mml:mi>b</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> is examined in Minkowski space where salient features of this field can be explored. In particular, we study the radiative degrees of freedom of this field while in a Minkowski space background. We show that it can be decomposed into irreducible representations, corresponding to tensor, vector, and scalar radiating solutions. Furthermore we examine geodesic deviation in the context of TW gravity about a Minkowski space background. We do this both at zeroth and first order in metric fluctuations <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>h</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mi>a</mml:mi><mml:mi>b</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>. We discuss that response of a gravitational wave antenna to the geodesics deviations. Measurements of gravitational waves by detectors like LIGO, VIRGO, KAGRA, LISA as well as Pulsar Time Arrays (PTAs) have constrained the presence of nontransverse-traceless modes in other theories of gravity. Future work could similarly be done to constrain the diffeomorphism field modes in TW gravity.

</details>

Abstract: The strongest experimental evidence for dark matter is the Galactic Center gamma-ray excess observed by the Fermi telescope and even predicted prior to discovery as a potential dark matter signature via weakly interacting massive particle dark matter self-annihilations. However, an equally compelling explanation of the excess gamma-ray flux refers to a population of old millisecond pulsars that also accounts for the observed boxy morphology inferred from the bulge old star population. We employ a set of Milky Way-like galaxies found in the HESTIA constrained simulations of the local universe to explore the rich morphology of the central dark matter distribution, motivated by the GAIA discovery of a vigorous early merging history of the Milky Way galaxy. We predict a significantly nonspherical gamma-ray morphology from the weakly interacting massive particle interpretation. Future experiments, such as the Cherenkov Telescope Array, that extend to higher energies, should distinguish between the competing interpretations.

Conclusion: 作者明确支持的应用为：所提出的公式可应用于 PeV 宇宙线在其源周围（PeVatrons）的传播、超高能宇宙线的传播，以及脉冲星周围 TeV 伽马射线晕的描述。关于这些应用是否直接涉及脉冲星、磁星、快速射电暴（FRB）、单脉冲发射、脉冲轮廓演化、模式转换、计时、偏振或射电频谱的具体物理结论，根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: We propose a brand-new formalism for the propagation of relativistic cosmic ray (CR) particles. The propagation of CRs has often been described using the diffusion approximation, which has the drawback that the propagation speed of CRs near the source exceeds the speed of light. By applying the analytic solution of the time-dependent distribution function of photons propagating while undergoing scattering, which we recently proposed, we have succeeded in formulating the propagation of relativistic CRs while preserving causality. The obtained formulas give correct expressions both in the diffusion regime and the ballistic regime, as well as the transition between them. They can be applied to the propagation of PeV CRs around their sources (PeVatrons), the propagation of ultrahigh energy CRs, and the description of TeV gamma-ray halos around pulsars.

</details>

Abstract: With the accumulation of pulsar timing data, the evidence of stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB) in the nanohertz is becoming more and more significant. It is gradually becoming possible to study the theory of gravity with pulsar timing data. Since pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) can observe multiple pulsars at the same time, their different spatial correlation patterns caused by SGWB with various polarizations make it possible to detect gravitational-wave polarization. In this paper, we investigate alternative polarizations of the SGWB in the NANOGrav 15-year dataset (NG15). First, the frequentist optimal statistic (OS) approach is generalized to accommodate frequency-dependent overlap reduction function (ORF), enabling OS analysis for all polarizations including scalar-longitudinal (SL) mode. We perform the first OS analysis for SGWB with all the six polarizations in NG15. The scalar-transverse (ST) mode has comparable signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) as the tensor-transverse (TT) mode, while the SNRs for vector-longitudinal (VL) mode and SL mode are much lower. Furthermore, Bayesian analysis is used for comparison purposes. The conclusion is in good agreement with the OS analysis, and the median value with 68% credible interval for TT and ST modes are <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>log</mml:mi><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>A</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>TT</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>14.6</mml:mn><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>9</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>1.65</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.29</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>log</mml:mi><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>A</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>ST</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>15.3</mml:mn><mml:msubsup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>8</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>1.72</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>0.70</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, respectively, while the upper limits at 95% confidence level for VL and SL modes are <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>log</mml:mi><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>A</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>VL</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>&lt;</mml:mo><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>15.10</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> and <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>log</mml:mi><mml:msub><mml:mrow><mml:mi>A</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mi>SL</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub><mml:mo>&lt;</mml:mo><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>16.56</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, respectively.

### [131] [Collisions and circular motion of spinning magnetized particles orbiting magnetized Kerr black holes]()
*Jumaniyozov, Shokhzod,Ullah Khan, Saeed,Rayimbaev, Javlon,Ibragimov, Inomjon,Abdujabbarov, Ahmadjon,Stuchlík, Zdeněk*

TL;DR: 该工作利用MPD方程研究磁化Kerr黑洞附近自旋磁化粒子的圆周运动与碰撞，发现自旋-曲率和磁偶极相互作用会显著改变稳定轨道与吸积动力学，并可将碰撞质心能量放大到超过标准BSW过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自旋磁化粒子在强引力与电磁场中的行为，对理解黑洞附近天体物理过程很重要，尤其是毫秒脉冲星绕超大质量或中等质量黑洞运动的情形；现有工作需在同时考虑自旋-曲率与磁偶极相互作用时，考察黑洞自旋、外部磁场、粒子内禀自旋与磁偶极矩对圆周运动、ISCO及碰撞能量的影响。

Method: 摘要未说明具体的观测或数值方法细节；作者采用Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon（MPD）方程，推导支配自旋磁化粒子圆周运动的有效势，并研究在黑洞自旋、外部磁场以及粒子内禀自旋和磁偶极矩影响下的最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO），进而探讨这类高能粒子碰撞的质心能量。

Result: 推导出支配自旋磁化粒子圆周运动的有效势，并给出在黑洞自旋、外部磁场、粒子内禀自旋与磁偶极矩影响下的ISCO；结果表明，磁偶极矩与外部场的相互作用以及自旋-曲率相互作用显著改变稳定轨道，导致吸积动力学改变；对高能碰撞质心能量的探讨表明，自旋与磁相互作用可将碰撞能量放大到超出标准Bañados-Silk-West（BSW）过程的水平。

Conclusion: 作者认为自旋与磁相互作用对稳定轨道、吸积动力学及碰撞能量的影响，对超高能宇宙线的产生以及活动星系核和低光度星系中喷流的形成具有意义；这些发现可能提供可利用强磁场中致密天体观测（例如超大质量黑洞附近的脉冲星）检验的理论预言。摘要提及毫秒脉冲星绕黑洞运动的情形，但其与脉冲星、磁星或快速射电暴观测的直接联系根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Studying spinning magnetized particles in strong gravitational and electromagnetic fields is crucial for understanding astrophysical processes near black holes, particularly in the scenarios of millisecond pulsars orbiting supermassive or intermediate-mass black holes. In this work, we analyze the circular motion and collisions of such particles in the close environment of a magnetized Kerr black hole, considering both spin-curvature and magnetic dipole interactions. Using the Mathisson-Papapetrou-Dixon (MPD) equations, we derive the effective potential governing the circular motion of spinning magnetized particles and investigate the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) under the influence of black hole spin, external magnetic fields, and intrinsic particle spin and magnetic dipole moment. The results reveal how the interaction of the magnetic dipole moment with the external field and spin-curvature interactions significantly alter stable orbits, leading to modifications in accretion dynamics. Furthermore, we explore the center-of-mass energy of such high-energy particle collisions, demonstrating that spin and magnetic interactions can amplify collision energies beyond the standard Bañados-Silk-West (BSW) process, with implications for the production of ultrahigh-energy cosmic rays and the formation of jets in active galactic nuclei and low-luminosity galaxies. Our findings may provide theoretical predictions that can be tested using observations of compact objects in strong magnetic fields, such as pulsars near supermassive black holes.

</details>


### [132] [Exploring the role of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>d</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>*</mml:mo></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> hexaquarks on quark deconfinement and hybrid stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.00981)
*Celi, Marcos O.,Mariani, Mauro,Kumar, Rajesh,Bashkanov, Mikhail,Orsaria, Milva G.,Pastore, Alessandro,Ranea-Sandoval, Ignacio F.,Dexheimer, Veronica*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 在 CMF 模型中引入 $d^*(2380)$ 六夸克态会显著改变混合星的状态方程，使高密度下软化并降低最大恒星质量，同时延迟退禁闭相变并减小能量密度间隙；要同时满足六夸克态存在与夸克退禁闭并符合天体物理约束，需要精细调节参数，而 SSHS 分支在其中起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探讨六夸克态 $d^*(2380)$ 这类奇异粒子在致密物质中的作用，以及其对混合星状态方程和向夸克物质退禁闭相变的影响；此前模型需重新参数化以与包括大质量脉冲星观测和引力波事件在内的近期天体物理约束相容。

Method: 采用手征平均场模型（CMF）研究 $d^*(2380)$ 六夸克态对混合星致密物质状态方程的影响，将六夸克态作为强子相中的新自由度引入，并重新参数化 CMF 模型以与近期天体物理约束相容。摘要未说明具体的观测数据、仪器、频段、时间跨度、样本量或统计分析方法。

Result: $d^*$ 的存在显著改变 EOS，导致高密度下软化并降低预测的最大恒星质量。六夸克态可延迟退禁闭相变并减小相关能量密度间隙，影响混合星的结构与稳定性。六夸克态的出现倾向于使恒星构型失稳，因此需精细调节模型参数才能同时获得六夸克态存在与夸克退禁闭；该情形下 SSHS 分支对获得满足当前天体物理约束的含六夸克态混合星起关键作用。

Conclusion: 作者认为，六夸克态 $d^*$ 等奇异粒子在致密物质中具有重要作用，强子自由度与夸克自由度在致密星体内部存在复杂的相互作用；由于六夸克态倾向于使恒星构型失稳，需要精细调节模型参数，并借助 SSHS 分支，才能获得同时包含六夸克态和夸克退禁闭且满足当前天体物理约束的混合星。根据摘要无法判断该工作与脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、快速射电暴等射电观测主题的具体关联。

Abstract: We investigate the impact of the <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mi>d</mml:mi><mml:mo>*</mml:mo></mml:msup><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>2380</mml:mn><mml:mo>)</mml:mo></mml:math></inline-formula> hexaquark on the equation of state (EOS) of dense matter within hybrid stars (HSs) using the chiral mean-field model (CMF). The hexaquark is included as a new degree of freedom in the hadronic phase, and its influence on the deconfinement transition to quark matter is explored. We reparametrize the CMF model to ensure compatibility with recent astrophysical constraints, including the observation of massive pulsars and gravitational wave events. Our results show that the presence of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mi>d</mml:mi><mml:mo>*</mml:mo></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> significantly modifies the EOS, leading to a softening at high densities and a consequent reduction in the predicted maximum stellar masses. Furthermore, we examine the possibility of a first-order deconfinement phase transition within the context of the extended stability branch of slow stable HSs (SSHSs). We find that the presence of hexaquarks can delay the deconfinement phase transition and reduce the associated energy density gap, affecting the structure and stability of HSs. Our results suggest that, as the hexaquark appearance tends to destabilize stellar configurations, fine-tuning of model parameters is required to obtain both the presence of hexaquarks and quark deconfinement in these systems. In this scenario, the SSHS branch plays a crucial role in obtaining HSs with hexaquarks that satisfy current astrophysical constraints. Our work provides new insights into the role of exotic particles like <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msup><mml:mi>d</mml:mi><mml:mo>*</mml:mo></mml:msup></mml:math></inline-formula> in dense matter and the complex interplay between hadronic and quark degrees of freedom inside compact stellar objects.

</details>


### [133] [Bounding anisotropic Lorentz invariance violation from measurements of the effective energy scale of quantum gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02883)
*Guerrero, Merce,Campoy-Ordaz, Anna,Potting, Robertus,Gaug, Markus*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该工作提出了一种将一般 LIV 框架中的有效量子引力能标 $E_{QG,n}$ 限制系统转换为 SME 光子扇区系数限制的一致方法，并据此把非双折射、$CPT$ 守恒的 SME 系数 $c_{(I)jm}^{(6)}$ 的界限改进了约一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIV 文献中关于 $E_{QG,n}$ 的一般性限制已充分确立，但很少被转换为标准模型扩展（SME）等替代理论框架的具体系数；尤其对 $n=2$ 的二次情形，现有 $E_{QG,2}$ 界限尚未被系统地转化为对非双折射、$CPT$ 守恒 SME 系数的限制，这一知识缺口促使作者开展本工作。

Method: 摘要说明：简要概述相关 SME 形式体系，并引入从 $E_{QG,2}$ 到 SME 参数的透明转换方法；综述基于飞行时间的最严格 $E_{QG,n}$ 界限，通过考虑系统误差、应用缺失的前置因子、并在需要时转换为双边高斯不确定度来进行标准化；再结合文献中的额外界限转换到 SME 光子扇区系数。具体观测数据、望远镜、频段、样本与时间跨度，摘要未说明。

Result: 摘要报告：标准化后的基于飞行时间的 $E_{QG,n}$ 限制与文献中的额外界限被用于改进 SME 光子扇区各个系数的界限，改进幅度约为一个数量级；作者开发了一套一致的方法学来完成从一般 LIV 框架到 SME 形式体系的转换。具体的数值界限、不确定度与统计显著性，摘要未说明。

Conclusion: 作者明确支持的核心结论是：通过一致的方法学，可以把一般 LIV 框架下的 $E_{QG,2}$ 界限系统地转换为对非双折射、$CPT$ 守恒 SME 系数 $c_{(I)jm}^{(6)}$ 的约束，并借助标准化后的限制及文献附加界限使这些 SME 系数界限改进约一个数量级。该结果对脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、模式转换或 FRB 的具体适用性，摘要未说明；若涉及时序或时间延迟类方法，其与脉冲星计时或瞬变射电观测的潜在联系根据摘要无法判断。

Abstract: Observations of energy-dependent photon time delays from distant flaring sources provide significant constraints on Lorentz invariance violation (LIV). Such effects originate from modified vacuum dispersion relations causing differences in propagation times for photons emitted simultaneously from gamma-ray bursts, active galactic nuclei, or pulsars. These modifications are often parametrized within a general framework by an effective quantum gravity energy scale <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>E</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mi>Q</mml:mi><mml:mi>G</mml:mi><mml:mo>,</mml:mo><mml:mi>n</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula>. While such general constraints are well established in the LIV literature, their translation into specific coefficients of alternative theoretical frameworks, such as the Standard-Model extension (SME), is rarely carried out. In particular, existing bounds on the quadratic case (<inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>n</mml:mi><mml:mo>=</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>) of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>E</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mi>Q</mml:mi><mml:mi>G</mml:mi><mml:mo>,</mml:mo><mml:mi>n</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> can be systematically converted into constraints on the nonbirefringent, <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>C</mml:mi><mml:mi>P</mml:mi><mml:mi>T</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>-conserving SME coefficients <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msubsup><mml:mi>c</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mi>I</mml:mi><mml:mo>)</mml:mo><mml:mi>j</mml:mi><mml:mi>m</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>(</mml:mo><mml:mn>6</mml:mn><mml:mo>)</mml:mo></mml:mrow></mml:msubsup></mml:math></inline-formula>. This work provides a concise overview of the relevant SME formalism and introduces a transparent conversion method from <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>E</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mi>Q</mml:mi><mml:mi>G</mml:mi><mml:mo>,</mml:mo><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> to SME parameters. We review the most stringent time-of-flight-based bounds on <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:msub><mml:mi>E</mml:mi><mml:mrow><mml:mi>Q</mml:mi><mml:mi>G</mml:mi><mml:mo>,</mml:mo><mml:mi>n</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:msub></mml:math></inline-formula> and standardize them by accounting for systematics, applying missing prefactors, and transforming results into two-sided Gaussian uncertainties where needed. We then use these standardized constraints, along with additional bounds from the literature, to improve bounds on the individual SME coefficients of the photon sector by about an order of magnitude. A consistent methodology is developed to perform this conversion from the general LIV framework to the SME formalism.

</details>


### [134] [Revisiting the search for dark matter subhalos using the Fermi-LAT 4FGL-DR4 catalog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.18736)
*Cheng, Ji-Gui,Zou, Le*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 利用Fermi-LAT 4FGL-DR4星表重新搜寻伽马射线暗物质子晕候选者，通过非分箱最大似然拟合识别出32个DM模型拟合优于经验函数的候选者，其$J$因子为0.2至$5.8\times 10^{20}\ \mathrm{GeV}^2\ \mathrm{cm}^{-5}$，DM粒子质量为30–500 GeV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值模拟提示，环绕星系的暗物质晕中存在众多小子晕，这些子晕可能可被Fermi LAT探测。本工作旨在利用最新的Fermi-LAT 4FGL-DR4星表重新审视对伽马射线子晕候选者的搜寻，以在该星表条件下重新考察子晕候选者。

Method: 利用最新的Fermi-LAT 4FGL-DR4星表，通过非分箱最大似然方法拟合星表中未关联点源的光谱数据。拟合中考虑两个模型：一是由星表提供的经验函数，二是暗物质模型，其中邻近子晕内的DM粒子湮灭为伽马射线和其他标准模型粒子。摘要未说明观测时间跨度、具体能段或其他分析细节。

Result: 基于拟合结果识别出32个候选者，其DM模型拟合的最大似然值超过经验函数拟合的最大似然值。这些候选者的估计$J$因子范围为0.2至$5.8\times 10^{20}\ \mathrm{GeV}^2\ \mathrm{cm}^{-5}$，DM粒子质量从30到500 GeV变化，其中12个处于$[30,80]\ \mathrm{GeV}$范围内。候选者4FGL $J2124.2+1531$为例外，其$J$因子为$4.52\times 10^{21}\ \mathrm{GeV}^2\ \mathrm{cm}^{-5}$，粒子质量为3108.44 GeV。所识别出的候选者与先前工作中报道的候选者不重叠。

Conclusion: 作者认为所识别出的候选者与先前工作中报道者不重叠，并讨论了造成这一差异的可能原因。他们指出，在当前阶段不能排除这些候选者是伽马射线脉冲星的可能性，需要通过多波段观测进一步确认。根据摘要无法判断这些候选者与快速射电暴或磁星的联系。

Abstract: Numerical simulations suggest that dark matter halos surrounding galaxies host numerous small subhalos, which might be detectable by the Fermi LAT. In this work, we revisit the search for gamma-ray subhalo candidates using the latest Fermi-LAT 4FGL-DR4 catalog. The search is performed by fitting the spectral data of unassociated point sources in the catalog through an unbinned maximum likelihood method. We consider two models in the fitting. One is an empirical function provided by the catalog, and another is a dark matter (DM) model in which DM particles within nearby subhalos annihilate into gamma rays and other Standard Model particles. Based on the fitting results, we identify 32 candidates for which the maximum likelihood value of the DM model fit exceeds that of the empirical function fit. The estimated <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>J</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>-factors of these candidates range from 0.2 to <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>5.8</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>20</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>GeV</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>cm</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>5</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula>, the DM particle masses vary from 30 to 500 GeV, and 12 of them are within the range of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mo>[</mml:mo><mml:mn>30</mml:mn><mml:mo>,</mml:mo><mml:mn>80</mml:mn><mml:mo>]</mml:mo><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mi>GeV</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>. Candidate 4FGL <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>J</mml:mi><mml:mn>2124.2</mml:mn><mml:mo>+</mml:mo><mml:mn>1531</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> is an exception, with a <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mi>J</mml:mi></mml:math></inline-formula>-factor of <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mn>4.52</mml:mn><mml:mo>×</mml:mo><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mn>10</mml:mn></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>21</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:mrow><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>GeV</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mn>2</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup><mml:mtext> </mml:mtext><mml:msup><mml:mrow><mml:mi>cm</mml:mi></mml:mrow><mml:mrow><mml:mo>-</mml:mo><mml:mn>5</mml:mn></mml:mrow></mml:msup></mml:mrow></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> and a particle mass of 3108.44 GeV. Interestingly, the identified candidates do not overlap with those reported in previous works, and we discuss the possible reasons for the discrepancy. At the current stage, we cannot rule out the possibility that these candidates are gamma-ray pulsars, and further confirmation through multiband observations is required.

</details>


### [135] [Generation of ultralight axions by a Kerr black hole. I. Why AGNs are favorable sources]()
*Pelletier, Guy*

Main category: scix

TL;DR: 该工作提出，类星体和活动星系核（AGNs）中由 Kerr 黑洞磁层内振荡的“gaps”产生强电场，可能有利地产生大量轴子，并估计了轴子产生效率，为类星体和 AGNs 周围晕中的热轴子暗物质组分提供了一种可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子受到粒子物理学和天体物理学关注，可能解决标准模型的一些困难，并可能是暗物质候选者、解决 ΛCDM 模型的问题。此前这类研究最初针对脉冲星，因为其磁场极强；但河外源更强大，更适合产生大轴子通量。摘要提出应考察类星体和 AGNs 是否可产生显著数量的轴子。

Method: 摘要说明本工作分两部分；这第一部分给出相互作用发生的天体物理条件，并估计轴子产生效率。所考虑的物理图像包括 Kerr 黑洞、其周围磁层、视界附近形成的“gaps”、强电场 \(E·B != 0\) 的非稳态区域、Blandford-Znajek 机制、Poynting 通量、Goldreich-Julian 电荷密度及非稳态屏蔽产生的振荡电场分量。摘要未说明具体观测、仪器、频段、时间跨度、样本或统计方法。

Result: 摘要报告，AGNs 的中心引擎是 Kerr 黑洞及其磁层，其中视界附近形成的“gaps”产生强电场并使 \(E·B != 0\)，尤其在非稳态区域有利于轴子产生；所关注的轴子其康普顿波长可与黑洞引力半径相当。能量来自黑洞旋转，经 Blandford-Znajek 机制驱动相对论性喷流和 Poynting 通量，并辅以 Goldreich-Julian 电荷密度及其非稳态屏蔽产生的振荡电场。轴子发射有两个通道：振动 gaps 内部产生轴子功率并产生前向和后向轴子波，以及轴子本身在 gaps 内对 Blandford-Znajek 光度的轻微提取。摘要未给出数值效率或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 作者提出，类星体和 AGNs 是产生大量轴子的有利源，上述过程为轴子粒子发射提供永久能量来源；这一第一部分估计了轴子产生效率，并给出一项可能性的希望，即形成类星体和 AGNs 周围晕中的热轴子暗物质组分。第二篇单独论文将详细研究带振荡 gaps 的 Kerr 黑洞磁层物理，特别是轴子如何参与 Blandford-Znajek 过程。根据摘要无法判断该机制与脉冲星、磁星、单脉冲、快速射电暴或其他射电观测之间是否存在直接联系。

Abstract: The hypothetical axion particles have received great attention in recent years in both particle physics, where they contribute to solving some difficulties with the Standard Model, and in astrophysics, where they could be a serious candidate for dark matter and solve problems with the <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mrow><mml:mi>Λ</mml:mi><mml:mi>CDM</mml:mi></mml:mrow></mml:math></inline-formula> model. We expect that the most powerful radiation sources in the Universe---quasars and, more generally, active galactic nuclei (AGNs)---could produce a significant amount of axions. The central engine of these high-energy objects is a Kerr black hole surrounded by a magnetosphere, where structures called ``gaps'' form close to the black hole horizon and generate intense electric fields such that <inline-formula><mml:math><mml:mover><mml:mi>E</mml:mi><mml:mo>--&gt;</mml:mo></mml:mover><mml:mo>·</mml:mo><mml:mover><mml:mi>B</mml:mi><mml:mo>--&gt;</mml:mo></mml:mover><mml:mo>!=</mml:mo><mml:mn>0</mml:mn></mml:math></inline-formula>; such a configuration is favorable for the production of axions, especially in the unsteady regime. First investigations of this sort have been performed for pulsars because of their very intense magnetic fields; however, extragalactic sources are far more powerful and more suitable to produce a large flux of axions particles. Moreover, the axions of interest would have a Compton wavelength comparable with the gravitational radius of their black hole. The primary energy supply stems from the rotation of a black hole threaded by a magnetic field, which powers a Poynting flux through the Blandford-Znajek mechanism that drives relativistic jets, which is supplemented by the Goldreich-Julian generation of an electric charge density whose unsteady screening generates a strong oscillating electric field component aligned with the main magnetic field that threads the black hole. Both processes provide a permanent source of energy for the emission of axion particles through two channels: the internal generation of axion power in vibrating gaps which generates forward and backward axion waves, and the slight extraction of the Blandford-Znajek luminosity by the axions themselves inside the gaps. The work is presented in two parts. In this first part, the astrophysical conditions under which the interaction takes place are presented and the efficiency of axion production is estimated, offering some hope for a cosmological solution in the form of a hot axion dark matter component in halos surrounding quasars and AGNs. In the second part (a separate paper), a detailed investigation of the physics of a Kerr black hole magnetosphere with the production of oscillating gaps reveals their capacity to be a powerful source of axions, in particular how axions participate in the Blandford-Znajek process.

</details>
